### 2026-10-18 12:45 CST — Claude: 1.20.1 Create gears, Viltrumite shaders/impacts, modding API

**Owner report** (Prism "Project ZombCraft 1.20.1" with `create-fabric-6.0.8.1+build.1744-mc1.20.1` (bundles Flywheel 1.0.5) and `viltrumitecore-1.11.0` + `viltrumiteflight-1.6.8`): (1) Create gears duplicated; (2) Viltrumite powers give a black screen, or Minecraft shaders do not work with PZ; Viltrumite explosion powers should destroy PZ objects; (3) wants a modding API so Minecraft mods work well with PZ. Owner said not to test; owner tests. Causes found by disassembling the owner's jars (scratchpad only).

**Causes and fixes (all version-local to 1.20.1 except the API):**
- Gears: Create's cogwheel model is a Fabric-renderer-API model (`BracketedKineticBlockModel`): the chunk renderer emits only the bracket, its vanilla `getQuads` fallback returns the whole cog. `BlockSceneExporter` read `getQuads`, so PZ drew a still cog, and Flywheel drew the turning cog into the video layer. Now non-vanilla-adapter models (`FabricBakedModel.isVanillaAdapter()==false`) are exported through `ModelBlockRenderer.tesselateBlock` (Indigo's hook) into `FluidCapture` (light from the vertices). `FlywheelVisuals120Mixin` (@Pseudo on `VisualizationManagerImpl.supportsVisualization`) returns false while `Session.nativeWorld`, so Create falls back to its block entity renderers, which `EntitySceneExporter` already captures each frame as native geometry. `FlywheelCompat120.frame` (from `LevelRenderer120Mixin` renderLevel HEAD) calls Flywheel's `resetAll()` + `levelRenderer.allChanged()` when that state flips.
- Black screen: Viltrumite Core runs its own `PostChain` (`viltrumitecore:shaders/post/dash_impact.json`, `ViltrumiteShaders.process`, log "Post zinciri kuruldu"), not through GameRenderer, so the old `PostEffect120Mixin` skip missed it. That mixin is removed. `PostChain120Mixin` wraps every `PostChain.process` that writes its screen target: `PostAlpha120` copies the target aside (blit) before the chain and writes its alpha back afterwards (raw GL program, colour mask alpha only), so effects run (hand/entities/VFX changed, additive parts added over PZ, PZ's own pixels cannot be distorted). If the restore fails it falls back to skipping the chain. Entity outline chain (writes `final`) untouched. Viltrumite flight's sonic_boom now also runs instead of being skipped.
- Impacts: Viltrumite punch/super punch/thunderclap/hurled mobs loop over blocks and break the non-air ones; PZ objects are air to Minecraft. `ViltrumiteImpacts120Mixin` (@Pseudo on `PunchImpactManager`, `ChargePunchManager`, `ThunderClapManager`; `executePunch`, `tickMeteorPhysics`, `executeSuperPunch`, `tickThunderclaps`) wraps their `ServerLevel.getBlockState` calls; `ModImpacts120` grows a box per call and sends it on return as a PZ blast via `PzCraftApi.breakArea` (ExplosionBridge trace). Meteor and punch `Level.explode` already reached PZ through `Explosion120Mixin`. `ExplosionBridge` now skips its INFO log for blasts radius < 3 that hit nothing (per-tick ability blasts).
- API: new shared `fabric-mod/src/main/java/pzcraft/api/PzCraftApi.java` (VERSION 1): `isPzConnected`, `isNativeWorld`, `blast(level, center, radius, fire)`, `breakArea(level, AABB)`, `hasPzObject(x,y,z)`, with notes for mod authors (PZ objects are air to MC; native drawing only sees block models and (Block)EntityRenderers; post chains cannot change PZ's pixels).

**Build:** `gradlew -Pmc1201 :mc1.20.1:build` PASS; jar `Project-ZombCraft-Fabric-0.16.2+mc1.20.1.jar` SHA256 `58905f13fba5612e057c9c3b59792150c24f3bc948a657a0cae3fcddb7974019` (1089409 B). Remapped mixin targets checked with javap: `class_3218;method_8320` (matches Viltrumite's call sites), `class_279.method_1258`, `supportsVisualization`. This build also contains the 12:00 PZ-mesh weapon path from source. 26.3 / 26.1.2 builds NOT rebuilt (the shared API and ExplosionBridge log change compile there too but were not built).

**Install:** INSTALLED in Prism "Project ZombCraft 1.20.1" mods (replacing `45d79768…`). Backup `E:/PzCraftTest/claude-create-viltrum-20261018/before-install`. PZ mod unchanged. No game was running; none launched.

**Not verified (owner testing):** nothing run in game: no mixin audit, no Create/Viltrumite session. Unknowns: Indigo shading/AO on exported Create bracket models may look slightly darker than vanilla blocks; post effects that brighten black (invert) wash PZ additively; ability blast radii (box half × 0.75 + 0.5) are estimates. No commit.

**Next:** owner plays Prism 1.20.1 + PZ with Create gears (expect one turning cog, depth-correct) and Viltrumite dash/punch/flight (expect no black screen) and punches/thunderclap next to PZ walls and furniture; adjust from the owner's report and `latest.log` lines "Flywheel visualization off", "Post effect … runs over PZ's world", "explosion at …".

### 2026-10-18 13:05 CST — Claude: 1.20.1 Viltrumite flight pose, grab, ragdoll pushes

**Owner report** after playing the 12:45 jar (log 12:47-12:51: both Viltrumite post chains ran "alpha kept", Flywheel off, Viltrumite punch boxes sent PZ blasts that broke floors/edges): (1) Viltrumite animations missing, especially flight; (2) cannot hold zeds with Viltrumite powers; (3) explosive powers should ragdoll zeds like TNT. Owner tests; nothing run here.

**Causes and fixes:**
- Flight pose: `viltrumiteflight` `SlowFlyingRendererMixin` (PlayerRenderer.setupRotations TAIL) treats `RenderSystem.getProjectionMatrix().m33() > .5` as the inventory paper doll and skips the flight transforms. `EntitySceneExporter.frame` runs after the HUD (orthographic projection), so Steve's native capture never got the pose. The capture now backs up the projection, sets a 70° perspective, restores it in `finally`.
- Grab: Viltrumite's `tickGrab` selects any living entity in a 1.5 box (proxies qualify), tags it `ViltrumiteGrabbed` and setPos's it to the hand each tick; `ActorProxies.syncActors` snapped it back to PZ's zombie. Proxies with that tag (or new `PzCraftApi.HELD_TAG` "pzcraft.held") are now treated like vehicle passengers: Rider with vehicle -1, PZ's `PassengerBridge` moves the zombie to the hand and clears its target; PZ attacks from held proxies are dropped; NoAi re-asserted every sync.
- Area hits: `allowDamage` applied Steve's melee reach / LOS / swing cap to every player-sourced hit, so thunderclap/super punch at range were rejected. New 1.20.1 `PlayerMelee120Mixin` counts `Player.attack` on the server (`ActorProxies.meleeDepth`, reset each tick, `meleeTracked` true only on 1.20.1 via preprocessor); those checks and `PzWeapons.forwardMelee` now apply to swings only. Non-swing player hits on non-held proxies are sent as explosion hits (PZ knock-down/ragdoll, `BlastRagdolls` on death), centre = attacker.
- Pushes: a proxy whose velocity (only ever set by others) is ≥1.0 blocks/tick horizontal or ≥0.8 up is "flung": a Hit with negative damage (= force, 0.5..3) and explosion=true is sent; skipped within 2 ticks of an explosion hit on that proxy. Also on release from a grab (throw), which ends the 10-tick dismount pose. PZ `Gameplay.applyHits`: negative damage = no health loss, hit force = -damage (old PZ jars treat it as a 0.05 hit).
- Log: `PostChain120Mixin` now logs each effect name once (12:48 log repeated it every frame).
- API v2: `PzCraftApi.HELD_TAG`, notes on stand-ins.

**Build:** `fabric-mod gradlew -Pmc1201 build :mc1.20.1:build` PASS (26.3, 26.1.2, 1.20.1); `protocol test` PASS; `pz-mod build` PASS. Installed only Prism 1.20.1 and PZ (26.x Prism instances unchanged; their builds now include the shared ActorProxies/API changes with meleeTracked=false = old behaviour).

**Not verified:** nothing run. Unknowns: whether Viltrumite core's other pose mixins have similar GUI checks (only the flight renderer reads the projection); a held zed during Steve's flight above PZ's top storey; Viltrumite meteor-physics on flung proxies now stops at once in MC (PZ's ragdoll carries the motion); thresholds are estimates.

**Next:** owner checks third-person flight pose, grab+carry+throw a zed, thunderclap/charge punch on zeds at range, punches killing zeds (ragdoll corpse). Logs: "PZ actor N flung", "explosion at".

Confirmed boundaries: tryFire/actionFire, tryReload/actionReload, PhasePreservingShotScheduler, prediction reconciliation and draw/hide/swap states. Full GunItem also contains excluded crafting/trading systems and must not be transplanted whole.

Jar metadata declares Loader >=0.19.2, Fabric API >=0.149.1+26.1.2 and GeckoLib >=5.5.2. The mashup uses 26.3 and its changed renderer; that metadata proves neither compatibility nor an installed dependency. As of 14:24, five hash-pinned M9 visual files are in the private generated pack, rendered with installed GeckoLib 5.5.7; no Point Blank mod code or sounds installed. Single-gun visual spike passes texture/clip capture; native M9 mechanics verified at 14:47–14:51, wider gun coverage remains open.

## 12. Document migration and authority

Consolidated sources: README.md, FEATURE-PLAN.md, plan-new.md, PLAN-final.md, PLAN.md, TESTING.md, MODLOG.md and POINTBLANK-ADAPTATION.md; owner's session screenshots; current source/builds; raw tests and logs. Original documents are byte-preserved in the section-4 ZIP.

- **HANDOFF.md:** current engineering plan, decisions, evidence status and journal. Edit this for ongoing work.
- **README.md:** concise player/runtime guide. Keep its user-facing behavior consistent with this file.
- **AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md:** small entry points directing both agents here; not competing progress logs.
- **Old plan/test/log markdown filenames:** retained as redirects so old references still resolve. Do not write new state into them.
- **Generated runtime logs/tests/screenshots:** retained in place as evidence. Their existence alone does not imply a pass.

If a future build or owner report contradicts this file, record the new verified observation, retain the previous evidence and update the resume point. Never silently convert an unverified feature, failed script or interrupted run into a completed milestone.

### 2026-10-05 ~21:10 CST — Claude — recoil port (gun owner item #8): implemented and built, NOT yet tested in game
Request: "time to continue features creation". Both games closed at the start (pz_status: no PZ/MC/gradle processes).
Changed: new `fabric-mod/.../GunRecoil.java` = single-shot port of Point Blank's `PlayerRecoilController` (M9: amplitude 3.0, halved aimed, yaw bias .18, duration 100 ms x7 clock, forward phase = (1-sqrt2/2)/7 of it, return `1-(1-t)^2.35`, immediate-return path 75 ms after the shot with 0.9..1.0 duration scale >= 300 ms, mouse movement during the kick shrinks what is returned, burst/`resetRatio` bookkeeping). Because PZ's mouse look is absolute input rewritten into the player every frame, the recoil is an **offset on that input**: `FrameHook.beforeFrame` now sets the player's look to `GunRecoil.yaw/pitch` (= input + offset) so the next shot and the camera follow the kicked view (old behaviour: camera-only kick, aim unchanged). `FrameHook.afterFrame` no longer adds kick; `GunFeel` lost kickPitch/kickYaw and the `recoil.pitch/yaw/decay/adsDamp` tuning keys (new keys: `recoil.amplitude`, `adsMultiplier`, `durationMs`, `completeMs`, `maxPitch`, `yawBias`, `burstGapMs`); `GunFeel.stats()` now reports `recoilPitch/recoilYaw/recoilActive`. A leftover offset (mouse took part of the return) settles with exp(-6 t) instead of persisting.
Assumptions to check against the owner's Point Blank feel: the reference's completeFiring timing (I used 75 ms = one 800 rpm cycle) and `Config.resetAutoFirePitchEnabled` default (only matters for >1 uninterrupted shots; M9 is SINGLE).
Build: `fabric-mod build prepareDirectLaunch --offline` BUILD SUCCESSFUL -> `pzcraft-fabric-0.13.0.jar` sha256 `5de4ca2d01af991a688c8365efeb550d38c0c46a26281a26411a88cbfe208c7e`. PZ jar unchanged `c9d7ef9a…` (installed = built). Protocol tests not rerun (no protocol change).

Kinds: m9 (Pistol), m1911a1 (Pistol2), deserteagle (Pistol3), rhino (4 revolvers incl. cap gun), m16a1 (AssaultRifle), mk14ebr (AssaultRifle2, JS14, TrapperCarbine), m870 (Shotgun, sawn-off), m590 (JS3T), c14 (HuntingRifle, VarmintRifle, cap rifle), l96a1 (MSR7T), citoricxs (both double barrels). Clips get logical names; derived clips are cut from the gun's own: `rack` (pump/bolt stroke from the fire clip, slide/charging part of the empty reload for magazine guns), `loadstart`/`load`/`loadend` (pump: prepare/load/finish; double barrel: open, both shells, close; revolver: cylinder out + cases out, loader in/out, close; bolt: raise + open bolt, hand trip, close). Shared running clips are merged in where a gun has none. `reloadPhase`/`reloadEmptyPhase` = Point Blank's reload-phase lengths.
- `tools/vic-guns/geoview.py` + `pngio.py`: offline renderer of a GeckoLib model and clip (GeckoLib 5's own transform rules, studied from its loader; numpy + zlib only), contact sheets from side/top/first-person views. Used to read clip timings without the game.
- Fabric: `VicGuns` (reads + verifies the manifest, registers `pz_m9` and `pz_gun_<kind>` for a fixed, only-growing id list so saved stacks keep their item, per-PZ-type item definitions `pzcraft:vic/<type>` that show PZ's sprite in slots and the gun in hands), `VicGunItem` (successor of `M9Item`, which is kept at `.pzcraft-backups/retired-source-20261007/M9Item.java.txt`): one clip controller per gun; PZ answers -> clips (`fired`, `reload started` at phase/seconds speed, `loading` -> loadstart then one `load` per PZ round interval ending when PZ inserts the round, end -> loadend (+ queued rack), `racking`/`racked`, `inspecting`); `magazine2` (the NEW magazine of Point Blank's reload clips) is no longer hidden, the magazine bones are hidden when PZ says the gun has no magazine; casings only for self-loaders (pistol/rifle case models); bigger flash for shotguns/rifles; `debug.clipTime` tuning key freezes a clip at a time. `PzItems.asGunItem` turns an existing `pz_item` firearm stack into its gun item (same type/state/id); `PzWeaponModels.retarget` leaves gun items alone.

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — M9 hand rack (in source, offline preview only)
Owner report: "i have issue in m9 animation and models. racking animation doesnt racking the gun manually." Only this one item was listed.
**Found (offline, `geoview.py`):** (1) `magazine_rack` cut the M9's `rack` clip from the end of Point Blank's `reloadempty`: there the slide stands locked back and drops by itself at 0.95 s; as a clip from rest the slide opened with no hand on it (the left hand was still under the magazine well) and shut again. Point Blank's M9 has no clip in which a hand works the slide all the way; its `inspect` has a press check (4.08-4.58 s: left hand on the slide, slide back 4.55 of 6, let go). The M1911's `reloadempty` brings the hand up beside the slide stop (measured: hand at y 17.4, the slide's underside is 16) and the slide drops without being pulled; its `inspect` has the same press check (2.55), so it could get the same treatment by adding it to `HAND_RACKED` (not done: only the M9 was reported). The Desert Eagle has neither a hand at the slide nor a press check. (2) `VicGunItem.emptyHeld` was "no round in the chamber", so a gun with a loaded magazine and an empty chamber (a found gun) idled with the slide locked back, and R then racked a slide that was already back.
**Changed (all unverified in game):**
- `tools/vic-guns/build.py`: `remap` (part of a clip on a new time line), `join` (one clip then another with a gap), `in_order` (every channel's keys written in time order; GeckoLib 5 re-sorts but warns), `press_check`, `slide_stroke`, `around`/`eased`, `hand_rack`, `racked_reload`; `HAND_RACKED = ('m9',)`. NEW motion, noted in `slide_stroke`: the press check's hand pose, the slide pulled the full 6.0, the pause to look removed, the gun 5 units higher than the inspection holds it (the hand and slide were below the first-person frame), the hand swung out to its own side on the way. `rack` 1.27 -> 1.05 s (reach 0.2, pull 0.1, hold 0.06, shut 0.04, back at rest 0.71/1.05). `reloadempty` 1.9167 -> 2.1333 s: Point Blank's clip until the magazine is in (0.9033), 0.28 s to the slide, tug to 6.5, let go at 1.2433, shut 1.2833, at rest 1.9333. Manifest: `reloadEmptyPhase` 1.68 -> 1.9333 (**PZ's M9 empty reload is 0.25 s longer at Reloading 0**), `cues.reloadempty` `[[0.1,0],[0.6333,1],[1.1433,2],[1.4333,3]]` (was rack 0.9167, rest 1.1). Only `gun_m9.animation.json` changes (clips `rack`, `reloadempty`); every other kind's animation file is byte-identical to the set copied from Maple.
- `fabric-mod/.../VicGunItem.java`: `emptyHeld` = chamber empty AND no rounds in the magazine (slide-back `idleempty`/`drawempty` only then). Needed with the new `rack`, which starts from a shut slide. Applies to every kind with `idleempty` (M1911, Desert Eagle, MK14).
- `tools/vic-guns/gunviewer.py --set DIR`: preview another build of the set.

Fabric `gradlew compileJava --offline`: BUILD SUCCESSFUL (JDK 25). Sheets: `tools/vic-guns/viewer/m9-rack-new.png`, `m9-reloadempty-new.png`; viewer `tools/vic-guns/viewer/m9-viewer.html` rebuilt from the new clips (git-excluded, private).

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — magazine unload clip (in source, offline preview + builds only)
Owner: "can we have unload animation that ejects magazine". Before: shift+R (`EJECT_MAGAZINE`) took the magazine out at once with no clip; the magazine bones just disappeared.
**Changed (all unverified in game):**
- `tools/vic-guns/build.py` `magazine_unload` (+ `UNLOAD_CARRY/AWAY/BACK`, `MAGAZINE_FLUNG`): clip `unload` = the gun's own `reload` until the old magazine is clear of the grip; NEW from there: the hand takes it down and out of sight (0.22 s, 3 left / 18 down / 8 back), the magazine's scale goes to 0, the hand comes back empty (0.38 s) while the gun returns the way its reload ends; the reload's new magazine (`magazine2`) is never shown. Manifest: `unloadPhase` and `cues.unload` = when the magazine leaves (the reload's own magazine-out sound mark): m9 1.32 s / 0.3667, m1911a1 1.1867 / 0.35, deserteagle 1.27 / 0.3667, m16a1 1.2534 / 0.3333. **mk14ebr has no `unload`** (M1A, JS-14, Trapper: its reload knocks the old magazine away, measured 31 units from the hand; shift+R there is as before).
- PZ `GunBridge`: `Eject(request, itemId, at)`. An eject request that carries the clip's timing answers `eject started` (clip `unload`, `seconds` = phase x 0.8/ReloadSpeed), the gun is busy (`ejecting`) until the mark, then `ejectMagazine` + `magazine ejected` (still with `ejected: true`); a swap (CANCEL) before the mark leaves the magazine in; `eject cancelled` if it is gone by then. Without timing (mesh guns, MK14, public build) the old immediate path is unchanged. Stat `ejecting`. After the mark the gun takes any action at once (the clip's way back is only seen).
- Protocol `GunAction`: comment only (what an eject request's list carries); no format change.
- Fabric: `PzGuns.send` sends `timing(unload, unloadPhase)` with shift+R for a kind that has the clip; `VicGunItem`: `eject started` plays `unload` at phase/seconds, `magazine ejected` alone also plays it (old PZ jar), `eject cancelled` goes to idle, the magazine bones stay drawn during `unload`, `/pzgunanim unload`. **Slide rule changed again** (supersedes the `emptyHeld` line of the entry above): slide back when nothing is left to fire AND (the gun was just drawn, or a round was in the chamber one look earlier = the last round was fired); it shuts as soon as there is something to fire. So taking the magazine out of a gun with an empty chamber no longer throws the slide open (`hadChamber`). Known: re-drawing such a gun shows the slide back.
**Evidence (offline):** hand end to magazine centre while it is held: pistols 8-11 units (the hand is at the magazine's base), M16 11.0 constant; side/first-person sheets `tools/vic-guns/viewer/m9-unload-new.png`, viewer rebuilt (14 clips). Every kind's keyed channels in time order; kinds other than m9/m1911a1/deserteagle/m16a1 byte-identical. Protocol tests BUILD SUCCESSFUL; `pz-mod` `gradlew jar` BUILD SUCCESSFUL -> `PzCraft.jar` 355812 B sha256 `508e4643e16cfbe5615d4147e3bbea27758e3db16817f88bc0dae9dd7522428e`; Fabric `compileJava` BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
**Not done / to judge on Maple:** nothing installed or run. In first person the magazine leaves at the bottom edge of the screen (as in Point Blank's reload). The empty magazine that drops by itself after the last shot still just disappears (no clip). `check-guns.py` step 4 and `check-gun-eject.py` A wait for `magazine ejected` and should still pass (it now comes ~0.37 s later); not run.

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — M9 magazine and cartridge, rounds fed by hand (in source, offline preview + builds only)
Owner: "now we gonna need to make 3d model of magazine and bullet. animating the loading bullets into magazine. first m9". Before: a magazine in the hand was PZ's flat sprite; R with rounds in the off-hand loaded one round with no motion.
**Made (`tools/vic-guns/build.py`, section "a magazine in the hand"; nothing of it is Point Blank motion):**
- Kind `mag9mm` (source model `m9`, recipe `feed`, PZ type `Base.9mmClip`). Geometry `magazine_item_geo`: the M9 model's magazine body and base plate, plus a mouth made here (collar that squares off the slanted top, two feed lips over the back half, back wall, follower), bone `top` = the round lying in it (shown when the magazine holds any: `roundBones`), bone `round` = the same cartridge for the feeding hand (drawn only during `load`/`unloadround`: `clipBones`), and the gun's two arm bones; the magazine stands 6.5 up / 2 forward out of the fist (`MAGAZINE_OUT`). `cartridge`: three cubes (brass case with a primer on its head, bullet, nose), 1.5 x 1.5 x 3.5. Texture `paint_rounds`: the M9 texture with five 4x4 patches painted into free space at (96..120, 0..4) (brass, case head + primer, copper, mouth steel, follower). First-person placement `firstPerson` rotation [25,15,8] translation [-5,-16.5,-11.5] (closer and tilted so the mouth is looked into; found by rendering candidates).
- Clips: `idle`, `draw` 0.35 s, `load` 0.62 s (round picked up out of sight at 0.10, over the open front 0.26, laid on the round below 0.32, pressed down 0.38 while `top` sinks into the magazine, pushed back under the lips 0.44 = `loadMark`, hand back at rest 0.62), `unloadround` = `load` backwards (`unloadMark` 0.24). The hand's arm keys are computed so its end stays 3.2 left of the round (`hand_end`, uses `geoview`'s bone maths; `geoview.Model` now also takes a geometry object). Left-arm turn (-25,-30,-45) found by rendering candidates.
**Game code (unverified):**
- Fabric `VicGuns.IDS` += `mag9mm` (item `pz_gun_mag9mm`; the list only grows). The item/stack/pack code is type-agnostic: a `Base.9mmClip` stack becomes that item (`PzItems.asGunItem`), PZ's sprite in slots, the model in hands.
- Fabric `VicGunItem`: `Loading` carries the round's clip and its mark; a kind with `loadMark`/`unloadMark` gets its clip timed so the MARK (not the end) falls on PZ's round, up to 0.95 of PZ's interval, and the clip runs on to take the hand back (no `toIdle` on `loaded`); `unloading` plays `unloadround` when the kind has it. Guns (no marks) behave as before.
- Fabric `EntitySceneExporter`: with any rigged model in the main hand the first-person pass draws the main hand only (`handRenderSelection = RENDER_MAIN_HAND_ONLY`), so the rounds in the off-hand do not add a second left arm; the off-hand slot in the HUD is untouched.
- PZ `GunBridge.startLoading`: the `loading`/`unloading` answer of a magazine now carries `first` (0.5/ReloadSpeed) and `per` (0.55/ReloadSpeed). Nothing else about magazines changed.
**Caught and fixed here:** the first version named a texture rectangle `STEEL`, which silently replaced the lever gun's own `STEEL` and changed `gun_lever.geo.json`; renamed (`ROUND_*`, `MOUTH_*`). Checked by building the committed script on this PC and comparing: only the four animation files of the earlier entries differ, plus the four new `gun_mag9mm` files. (So the `gun_c14.png`/`gun_lever.png` byte differences against the set copied from Maple are this PC's PNG encoding, not a change.)
**Evidence (offline):** sheets `tools/vic-guns/viewer/mag9mm-load-new.png`; viewer `tools/vic-guns/viewer/mag9mm-viewer.html` (`gunviewer.py mag9mm --set DIR --views fp,right,top`; `gunviewer.py` got `--views` and no longer assumes a `reload` clip); Blockbench files in `viewer/mag9mm-blockbench/`. 17 kinds, 23 PZ types, 74 files. Protocol tests BUILD SUCCESSFUL; `pz-mod` jar BUILD SUCCESSFUL (`6ff8c7e8…ac22`, 355829 B); Fabric `compileJava` BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
**Not done / to judge on Maple:** nothing installed or run; a new registered item means the first launch re-registers items (the id list only grows, existing stacks keep theirs). Third person and the item on the ground were not looked at (the M9's display transforms are used). The left arm is large in first person at this distance (Steve's arm is 8 units wide, the magazine 2.9). Only the top round is shown, not how full the magazine is. Other magazines (.45, .44, 5.56, M14) still use PZ's sprite; `Base.Bullets9mm` in the hand is still a sprite. R still loads ONE round per press (`LOAD_ONE_ROUND`); holding to fill is not there.
**Next:** the Maple batch in §1; then the other magazines the same way (`magazine_item_geo` takes any gun model with `magazine` + `bullet` bones).

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — the empty magazine (in source, offline preview + compile only)
Owner: "should have other state that magazine is empty and no round is present in magazine". Before: with no rounds the `top` round was hidden by `roundBones`, but nothing stood in its place, and the viewer never showed it (it draws every bone).

The same two clips serve both cases: a round into an empty magazine pushes the follower down, the last round out lets it come up.
- Fabric `VicGuns.Kind.emptyBones` (drawn only while `heldRounds == 0`), `VicGunItem`: `saidRounds`: the count in PZ's `loaded…`/`unloaded…` answer is shown until the mirrored stack agrees (1 s at most), so the round/follower swap is not a few ticks late.
- `gunviewer.py`: a kind with `emptyBones` gets each clip twice (`idle (empty)`, `draw (empty)`, `load (first round)`, `unloadround (last round)`), switching at the clip's mark as PZ's count would; bones of `clipBones` are drawn only in their clips.
**Evidence (offline):** `tools/vic-guns/viewer/mag9mm-empty-new.png` (loaded and empty mouth, first round going in), viewer rebuilt (8 entries). Compared with a build of the committed script, the only changed files are still the four animation files of the earlier entries plus the four `gun_mag9mm` files. Fabric `compileJava` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. The PZ jar is unchanged by this entry (`6ff8c7e8…ac22`).
**To judge on Maple:** rounds fed one at a time by PZ without an answer in between (shift+R takes them all out, one per 0.69 s) rely on the mirrored stack for the last one: a tick or two of the wrong top may show; say so if it does.

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — M9 marked done; M1911 and B-F pistol get the same pass (in source, offline preview + compile only)
Owner: "mark m9 done. the procedures we've with m9 make it similar to m1911 and b-f pistol. note they cant share same model of magazine". Checklist: §6 "Per-gun pass".
**Found:** the three pistols share Point Blank's arm bones (same pivots and rest turns), so hand poses carry over. The M1911's `inspect` has the same press check as the M9's (slide 2.55 of 6). The B-F (`deserteagle`) has no press check, but its own `reloadempty` already has the left hand on the locked slide (1.2333-1.3 s) pulling away as the slide goes home (a slingshot), so that clip needed nothing; only its `rack` (cut from that clip) opened the slide with no hand on it. In the M1911/B-F `reloadempty` the right arm moves only after 1.08 s, later than the cut, so nothing is left hanging.
**Changed (`tools/vic-guns/build.py`):**
- `HAND_RACKED = {'m9': 'press', 'm1911a1': 'press', 'deserteagle': 'slingshot'}`. `press`: `rack` and the end of `reloadempty` from the gun's own press check (as the M9). `slingshot` (`slingshot_stroke`): `rack` from the gun's own hold on the locked slide, with the slide shut under the hand first and drawn back by it (NEW: the pull); `reloadempty` untouched. `RACK_SLIP` (the hand travels 0.75 less than the slide) replaces the M9-only arithmetic; `racked_reload` now reads "the new magazine is in" from the `magazine` position channel (the M1911's `magazine2` carries stray rotation keys to 1.98 s that pushed the cut past the slide drop). M1911: `rack` 1.55 -> 1.05 s, `reloadempty` 2.30 -> 2.1333 s, `reloadEmptyPhase` 2.02 -> 1.9333, rack cue 1.2 -> 1.1433. B-F: `rack` 1.57 -> 0.9126 s, the rest as Point Blank's.
- Magazines: `MAGAZINES` (one entry per magazine: PZ type, the gun model it is taken from, its mouth measured on that model, its cartridge, how far it stands out of the fist, free texture space) replaces the M9-only constants; `magazine_item_geo`, `cartridge`, `paint_rounds`, `recipe_feed` take the entry. `mag45` (M1911 body with witness holes, .45 ACP 1.8 x 3.8), `mag44` (B-F body and base plate, .44 Magnum 1.75 x 4.8 with a rim). Texture patches at (95,15) and (78,0) of each gun's own 120x120 texture. `mag9mm` is numerically the same as before (checked value by value).
- Fabric `VicGuns.IDS` += `mag45`, `mag44` (items `pz_gun_mag45`, `pz_gun_mag44`). No other code change; PZ jar unchanged (`6ff8c7e8…ac22`).
**Evidence (offline):** hand end against the slide: M1911 `rack` hand z -1.9 -> 3.9 with the slide 0 -> 6 (same as the M9), B-F `rack` hand at y 23.8 on the shut slide (slide 18.9-24.9), z 0.1 -> 5.3 with the slide, 12.8 after letting go. Sheets `tools/vic-guns/viewer/deserteagle-rack-new.png`, `m1911a1-reloadempty-new.png`; viewers `m1911a1-viewer.html`, `deserteagle-viewer.html`, `mag45-viewer.html`, `mag44-viewer.html`. 19 kinds, 25 PZ types, 82 files; against a build of the committed script only the m9/m1911a1/deserteagle/m16a1 animation files differ, plus 12 new magazine files. Every keyed channel of every kind in time order. Fabric `compileJava` BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
**Mistake caught here:** a first-person check of the two new magazines looked badly wrong because my shell command dropped the display file's name (`$k_base`); re-rendered with the right file, they sit like the M9's. Nothing in the build was wrong.
**Not done:** M16 and MK14 family (see the checklist). The B-F's `reloadempty` hand motion is Point Blank's own and was not restyled to match the M9's overhand pull. Nothing run in the game.
**Next:** the Maple batch in §1 now also covers the M1911 and the B-F pistol and their magazines; then the M16 and the MK14 family.

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — owner's two reports from the viewer: grip clipping in `inspect`, "M1911 has holes and gaps"
Owner: "have issues with grip of pistol. clipping problem" / "m1911 model have holes and gaps"; asked which, he said "look at inspect animation you'll see".
**Both were mostly the offline previewer, not the models. The game is not affected by either (GPU depth buffer, its own background).**
- **`geoview.py` first-person depth bug (FIXED).** Depth and texture were interpolated linearly on screen and faces were clamped, not cut, at the eye. A face that reaches towards the eye (the 24-long arm blocks) got depths that were far too large, so gun parts that are INSIDE or BEHIND the hand were drawn in front of it: in `inspect` the whole grip seemed to stand in front of the right hand. Proof it was the previewer: exact sight lines from the first-person eye to the M1911's grip panels at inspect 1.6 s / 3.2 s enter the right arm block at 0.68-0.98 of the way (hidden) while the old render drew them. Now: faces are cut at a near plane (0.05) and depth/texture are interpolated in 1/depth (`clip_near`, `NEAR`). **Every first-person render and viewer page made before this fix shows the bug**; the sheets `m9-rack-new.png`, `m9-reloadempty-new.png`, `m9-unload-new.png`, `mag9mm-*.png`, `mag45-mag44-new.png`, `deserteagle-rack-new.png`, `m1911a1-reloadempty-new.png` in `tools/vic-guns/viewer/` predate it (the clips themselves were re-checked with the fixed renderer and look right; side/top views were never affected).
- **Viewer background.** The M1911 is solid: an untextured render has no openings and no face maps to empty texture. Its "gaps" are near-black painted strips (the line between slide and frame, 27 faces averaging darker than 45) on a background of (38,40,46). `geoview.render(..., background=)` / `--bg`, and `gunviewer.py` now renders on (112,128,148) with gain 1.3; the page's stage has the same colour.
- **What is really there:** the right fist is an 8-thick block and the grips are about 13 tall, so only part of a grip is inside it: M9 58 %, M1911 53 %, B-F 42 % of sampled grip points (nothing comes out of the block's SIDES; the butt of the grip is below it, the top of the front strap past its end). That is Point Blank's hand placement and is how it looks in the game. Measured alternatives, NOT applied: same hand moved, at best 63-64 %; a hand 1.25x thicker 76-78 %; 1.4x 82-90 %.
- **B-F `inspect`/`inspectempty` (CHANGED, in source):** Point Blank moves the right arm 4 to the right and 10 degrees for 0.1-2.57 s to show the gun's side, which leaves the whole grip standing in front of the hand; `HAND_STAYS = ('deserteagle',)` drops that `rightarm` channel so the hand stays on the grip like the M9's and M1911's. Only `gun_deserteagle.animation.json` changes.
**Evidence:** `tools/vic-guns/viewer/inspect-before-after.png` (three pistols, old and fixed renderer), `deserteagle-inspect-hand.png`, `m1911a1-solid.png`; all six viewer pages rebuilt with the fixed renderer. No Java change; PZ jar still `6ff8c7e8…ac22`.
**Lesson for the previewer:** when a first-person render disagrees with geometry, check with sight lines before changing a pose; I spent a long search on hand placements that the bug made look necessary.

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — M16 pass
Owner: "let work with m16 first. i need to see its model and animations". Everything below is in source and previewed offline only; nothing installed, nothing seen in the game, **the owner has not looked at it yet**.
**What the M16 was before this entry (found from the viewer and the clip data):**
- Model: Point Blank's `m16a1` with its `m16a1kit` shown (triangular handguard, carry handle, 30-round magazine); `launcher`, `lasercannon`, `shotgun`, `_cb_m16a3kit`, `_cb_suppressor`, `scope` hidden. No change.
- `rack` (R with rounds in the magazine and none in the chamber) was `magazine_rack`: the tail of the empty reload, i.e. the left hand slapping the bolt release while the `bolt` bone stood at 7.9 for the whole clip (Point Blank keys it as a constant in `reloadempty` and shuts it by going back to idle). The charging handle (bone `action`) is not touched by any M16 clip of Point Blank. Same defect the owner reported on the M9.
- Point Blank holds its rifles with the left arm moved and scaled in `idle` (rotation -4.98/-5.05/-3.84, position 3.225/-4.85/0, scale 1.2/1.3/1.2), not at zero. Two clips made earlier did not know that: the old `rack` started from zero (a hop at its start) and `unload` (2026-10-08 "magazine unload clip") ended with the hand at zero and scale 1.22 (a hop when idle resumed).
**Changed (`tools/vic-guns/build.py`):**
- `CHARGED = {'m16a1': 'm4sopmodii'}`, `charging_stroke`, `charging_rack`: the M16's `rack` is the left hand leaving the handguard for the charging handle (NEW: that way there, 0.3 s, 6 out to the left half way), then **Point Blank's own M4 SOPMOD II pull** from its `reloadempty` (1.2833 s to 2.3 s of that clip): the M4 has the identical `action` bone (pivot [0,26,3], same four cubes) and the same arms; its arm bone is 0.475/0.45 off the M16's and turned 0.06/-0.25/1.58 degrees differently, which is corrected, and its idle pose is replaced by the M16's own. The M4's bolt stands back until the handle is let go (its magazine was empty); here the bolt is shut and goes with the handle (`bolt` = `action` keys, 7.4). Point Blank does the pull at the lower edge of the view and turns its `_camera_` bone down to it; Fabric does not use that bone, so the gun is raised 5 and pushed 5 forward while the hand is on the handle (`CHARGE_LIFT`, full until the handle is home, half at the next key). Measured on the built clip: hand end at (1.7, 25.7, 1.1) on the shut handle 0.30-0.40 s, at (0.8, 26.2, 8.4) with handle and bolt at 7.4 from 0.467 to 0.583 s, handle and bolt home at 0.60 s, hand back at the idle pose and gun at rest at 1.3167 s (clip length; was 1.67).
- `bolt_released`: the M16's `reloadempty` is Point Blank's own (the hand hits the bolt release); the bolt now stands back until that hit (the leftarm key nearest the gun within 0.15 s of the clip's action sound mark: 2.2 s) and is home 0.04 s later, instead of standing open to the end. Phase and cues unchanged (`reloadEmptyPhase` 2.9).
- `bolt_open`: new `idleempty` and `drawempty` for the M16 (the gun's `idle`/`draw` with the bolt at 7.9), so `VicGunItem`'s empty-gun rule (chamber and magazine empty) shows the bolt held open, as it does the pistols' slides. In first person the bolt is on the far side of the gun; it shows from the right.
- `at_rest(own)`: a bone's pose in the gun's idle clip. `magazine_unload` returns the hand to it (and to its scale); for the pistols, whose idle has no arm pose, the output is byte-identical.
- New kind `mag556` (source model `m16a1`, recipe `feed`, PZ type `Base.556Clip`): the M16 model's own magazine with a mouth, and a 5.56 cartridge (`neck`: case, neck, bullet, tip; 1.4 x 1.4 x 6.0, the size of Point Blank's own `bullet` bone). Two columns (`stagger` 0.7): `top` lies to the left, `next` beside and 0.5 below it, the follower is a low plate with a rib where the second round would be. Bones: `held` (holds a copy of the rib; parent of `top`), `next`, `follower`. `roundBones: ['held', 'next']` uses the rule Fabric already has (the n-th bone is drawn when the count is at least n): 0 rounds = plate and rib, 1 = one round beside the rib, 2 or more = two rounds. The left arm bone, which on the rifle is out at the handguard, is put where the pistols' is (`left`: the M9's left arm relative to its right arm). Texture: five 4x4 patches at (145..169, 1..5) of the M16's 500x500 texture (free space). First-person placement: the shared `MAGAZINE_VIEW`.
**Changed elsewhere:** `VicGuns.IDS` += `mag556` (item `pz_gun_mag556`; no other Java change); `gunviewer.py` hides the second and later round bones in the "first round"/"last round"/"empty" variants; `hand_end` takes the arm's scale.
**Evidence (offline):** build 20 kinds, 26 PZ types, 86 files; against the build before this entry only `gun_m16a1.animation.json` differs, plus the four new `gun_mag556` files. Sheets in `tools/vic-guns/viewer/`: `m16-rack-seq.png` (first person and left), `m16-rack-new-flat.png` (flat colours: the handle), `mag556-rest.png` (2, 1, 0 rounds), `mag556-load.png`, `mag556-load-first.png`. Viewers `m16a1-viewer.html` (13 clips, four cameras), `mag556-viewer.html`. Fabric `compileJava` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (needs `JAVA_HOME` = JDK 25; a plain `./gradlew` from Bash picks Java 17 and fails). No PZ or protocol change: PZ jar still `6ff8c7e8…ac22`.
**Not done / to judge on Maple:** PZ plays the rack sound at the request, the hand reaches the handle 0.3 s later (same question as the pistols); the M16 in third person and the bolt seen from the right; a `Base.556Clip` in the hand with `Base.556Bullets` in the off-hand (R feeds, shift+R takes out); the magazine's size in the first-person view (it is a third taller than the 9mm one, same placement). The M16's `inspect`, `reload`, `fire`, `draw` are Point Blank's own and were not changed. Nothing committed or pushed.
**Next step:** the owner looks at `m16a1-viewer.html` and `mag556-viewer.html`; then the same pass on the M1A / JS-14 / Trapper family (`mk14ebr`: its reload flings the magazine, so `unload` needs its own solution, and its three PZ guns take different magazines: `Base.M14Clip`, `Base.JS14_Clip`, and the Trapper the .45 one).

### 2026-10-08 — Claude (office PC) — no-magazine states
Owner, after the M16 pass was shown: "there will be more state: idlenomag, drawnomag, reloadnomag, inspectnomag, runningnomag, racknomag" (he first wrote `runnomag`, then replaced it with `racknomag`). In source and previewed offline only.
**Before:** a gun whose magazine PZ says is out (`g.clip` false) was drawn by a rule in `VicGunItem` (the `magazine`/`magazine2` bones hidden outside reload and unload clips). That showed nothing in the viewer, and R on such a gun played the ordinary reload: a hand taking out a magazine that is not there, and PZ's eject sound.
**Changed (`tools/vic-guns/build.py`, recipe `magazine`, all five kinds):**
- `without_magazine(clip, bones)`: `idlenomag`, `drawnomag`, `inspectnomag`, `runningnomag`, `racknomag` = the gun's clip with the magazine bones scaled to nothing. Same lengths.
- `reload_without_magazine(clip, phase, cues)`: `reloadnomag` and `reloademptynomag` from the gun's FINAL `reload`/`reloadempty` (so the M9's and M1911's hand rack and the M16's bolt release are in them). The part in which the old magazine comes out is cut (0.1 s to get from the pose before it to the pose after it). Two kinds of Point Blank reload: (a) the new magazine is the bone `magazine2`, brought up before the old one is taken out (pistols' `reload`, both M1A reloads): `magazine` is not shown, the cut is from the old magazine's last key in the gun to the new one's last key before it seats, and where the old one is carried away afterwards the left arm goes straight back instead; (b) the same bone goes out and comes back (M16, the pistols' `reloadempty`): it is shown from the last key at which it is furthest away, and the left arm's keys of the 0.3 s before the magazine left are dropped, so the hand goes down for a magazine without reaching for the magazine well first. Phase and sound marks move with the cut; marks of a magazine coming out (kind 0) are dropped.
- Numbers (clip length, then its phase, with the ordinary clip's phase in brackets): M9 `reloadnomag` 1.8833 s, 1.23 (reload 1.58), `reloademptynomag` 2.1167, 1.9167 (1.9333); M1911 1.8166, 1.1833 (1.6) and 2.1167, 1.9167; B-F 1.8833, 1.12 (1.47) and 2.2834, 1.9134 (1.93); M16 2.4333, 1.6033 (1.82) and 2.9666, 2.6833 (2.9); M1A 2.2166, 1.6866 (1.87) and 2.4999, 2.3666 (2.55).
**Changed (Fabric):** `VicGunItem.fitting(kind, clip)` gives `<clip>nomag` when the held gun has no magazine and the kind has that clip: used for idle, draw, reload started (with the no-magazine phase), rack/racked, inspect, and the run trigger (`runnomag` = `runningstart` then `runningnomag` looped). Slide-back clips still come first (`idleempty`, `drawempty`, `inspectempty`: an empty gun without a magazine uses those, and the old bone rule hides the magazine; the rule stays for every clip without a `nomag` twin, such as `fire`). `PzGuns`: a reload request for a gun without a magazine carries the timing of `reloadnomag`/`reloademptynomag`, so PZ's reload is that much shorter and plays no eject sound. `/pzgunanim` knows the new names. No PZ or protocol change.
**Evidence (offline):** every clip that existed before is byte-identical in all five animation files (compared clip by clip); only those five files changed. Sheets in `tools/vic-guns/viewer/`: `m16-reloadnomag.png`, `m9-reloadnomag.png` (first person and left), `nomag-check.png` (M16 and M9 `reloademptynomag`, M1A `reloadnomag`, `idlenomag` of M16 and M9). Viewer pages of `m16a1`, `m9`, `m1911a1`, `deserteagle` rebuilt (the new clips are at the end of the clip list). Fabric `compileJava` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. PZ jar still `6ff8c7e8…ac22`.
**Not done / open:** the M1A's two no-magazine reloads were built by the same rule but only glanced at (its pass has not started); `fire`, `runningstart`, `runningend`, `unload` have no `nomag` twin (bone rule). In game on Maple: the mirror's `g.clip` decides, so check shift+R then draw, run, inspect, X/R rack and R, each with and without a chambered round. Nothing committed or pushed.
**Next step:** the owner looks at the M16 page (`reloadnomag`, `reloademptynomag`, `idlenomag`, `drawnomag`, `inspectnomag`, `runningnomag`, `racknomag`), then the pistols' pages.

### 2026-10-09 — Claude (office PC) — viewer pages committed (owner's decision)
Owner: "yes commit them i've had hard time having 1:1 animation and model implementation in maple-pc". **Found:** the gun controller was built with a transition length of 0 (`new AnimationController<VicGunItem>(CONTROLLER, 0, …)`), so every change of clip (`triggerAnim` in `play`, `setAnimation` in `toIdle`) snapped. GeckoLib 5.5.7 (`AnimationController.setTransitionTicks`, `transitionFromPoint`) crossfades from the pose on screen over that many ticks; the value is read when the new clip starts (bytecode of `checkControllerState` and `initializeNewAnimation`).

**Changed (`fabric-mod/src/main/java/pzcraft/mc/VicGunItem.java`, live workspace only, not in `office-sync`):**
- Default blend 5 ticks. `blendTicks(clip)`: `fire` 2 (a shot answers at once), `rack*` 3, `draw*`, `load*`, `unloadround` 4, `reload*`, `unload`, `idle*` 6, `run*` and `inspect*` 8.
- `play()` and `toIdle()` set the controller's transition length before the trigger or switch.
- The round, magazine and clip-bone show/hide rules use `shownClip`, which follows `lastClip` only once the blend is done. A round or magazine therefore does not jump ahead of the hand carrying it. `lastClip` still drives the logic.
- `animationStats()` adds `transitioning`, `blendTicks` and `shownClip`; they appear in `state()['minecraftGui']['gunFeel']['animation']`.

### 2026-10-08 22:30 CST — Claude (Maple PC) — X rack and animation fixes (source only, not installed)

Owner, while playing: "animation states is not seamless", "some times that animation is jittering", "most animation isn't accurate to gun viewer we have", "also add X for racking".

**Found in the logs and code (not yet measured in game after these changes):**
- Jitter: the owner's repeated I presses sent `inspecting` 15 times in 27 s (`fabric-run-live.log`, 22:20). Each press restarted the inspect clip (6.6 s) from its first frame.
- Jitter/flicker: `GunFeel.sprinting()` is `gunHeld && isSprinting && onGround`, so a landing or a step can flip the run pose each tick.
- Accuracy: the game time-stretched clips to PZ's timers (`speed = phase/seconds` for reload and unload; `length/seconds` for rack and the loadend stroke: M9 rack 1.05 s authored vs PZ about 0.85 s, so about 24% faster than the viewer). The viewer plays every clip at 1.0.
- Seamless (open): bone show/hide rules switch at blend end (`shownClip`), and the no-magazine magazine rule switches at once when the mirror flips. Neither is verified in game yet.
- X: GLFW key 88 had no mapping. R, I and B are wired by key code in `GuiBridge` (Minecraft side) and intercepted in `GuiInput` (PZ side).

**Changed:**
- `protocol/.../GunAction.java`: `RACK = 9`. The validator's upper bound is now `RACK` (it was `FIRE_MODE`, which rejected the new code). `GunActionTest`: RACK is in the magazine-identity round trip; the malformed-request check uses `RACK + 1`, since 9 is now valid.
- `pz-mod/.../GunBridge.java`: `RACK` dispatches to `startRack(r, gun, false)`: the native rack (the spent round out, the next one in from the magazine), with `racking` then `racked`. Without this line an unknown action falls through to `fire()`, which would shoot.
- `pz-mod/.../GuiInput.java`: X is intercepted while a firearm is in hand and the game is not paused (same pattern as I and B).
- `fabric-mod/.../GuiBridge.java`: key 88 calls `PzGuns.rackKey`. `PzGuns.rackKey` requests `RACK`, and `send()` routes `RACK` through the same non-firing branch as `INSPECT` and `FIRE_MODE` (without this it would fire).
- `fabric-mod/.../VicGunItem.java`:
  - `play()`: an inspect that is still running is not restarted by another inspect.
  - Sprint: the run/idle pose changes only after `SPRINT_HOLD_TICKS` (5) ticks of the same flag.
  - Speed: reload, unload, rack, loadend and the queued rack play at 1.0. Loads still speed up to fit PZ's per-round time (never slower than the clip), and loadstart keeps its fit.

**Checks (isolated builds, so the running games' `build/classes` and `build/libs` were not touched):** protocol `test` 59 of 59 pass (after the two test fixes above); Fabric `compileJava` BUILD SUCCESSFUL; PZ `jar` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (`build-check/libs/PzCraft.jar` 355868 B). The installed PZ jar is still `6ff8c7e8…ac22`.

**Known trade-offs:** PZ still owns the sound timing, so a sound can land a little off a clip that now plays at its own pace. Tell me which sound if that happens.

**Not done:** install (needs a restart, so the owner's session must end), X test in game, and the seamless check. Plan after the restart: the four states at once (`pzgunanim` probes as before), the transition stats, and a side-by-side against the viewer for reload, rack and inspect.

- Aiming and animated bones: `VicGunItem.adjustRenderPose` aligns the sights with the model AT REST. GeckoLib 5.5.7 attaches the animated bone snapshots only inside `RenderPassInfo.renderPosed` (decompiled sources in `~/.pzcraft/private/geckolib-26.3`), which runs after `adjustRenderPose`. So anything a clip does to `main` is still seen while aiming (a shot's kick, and now the idle breath).

**Changed:**
- `tools/vic-guns/build.py`:
  - `MAGAZINE_VIEW` translation `[-5, -16.5, -11.5]` -> `[-1.5, -19.5, -23]` (rotation `[25, 15, 8]` kept; found by rendering candidates). Top round now 1.36 blocks from the eye (`mag9mm`) and 1.16 (`mag556`): about half the size on screen.
  - `under_view(view, point, left, clear)`: the nearest place under the lower edge of the first-person view, in the model's numbers. `recipe_feed` uses it for the round's pickup point (`FEED_LEFT`, `FEED_UNDER`; it was a fixed 9 units below the mouth, which the new view shows in mid-screen: the round popped into the hand in sight) and for where `draw` starts (`DRAW_UNDER`, `DRAW_TURN`, `DRAW_TIMES`; it was a fixed 9 units down). `FEED_TIMES` first two `(.10, .26)` -> `(.08, .27)` (the hand has further to go); `draw` 0.35 -> 0.45 s. `loadMark` 0.44 and `unloadMark` 0.24 are unchanged.
  - `charged_reload`: for a gun in `CHARGED` (the M16), `reloademptynomag` = the no-magazine empty reload until the magazine is seated (+0.07 s), 0.3 s for the hand to get from the magazine to the charging handle (NEW motion, `around` with `CHARGE_AROUND`), then `charging_stroke` (the stroke `rack` has). The bolt stands back (7.9) until the handle is let go and goes home with it. Length 2.9666 -> 3.0533 s, `reloadEmptyNoMagPhase` 2.6833 -> 2.8533, cues `[[0.7,3],[1.1666,1],[1.8966,2],[2.2866,3]]` (the action's mark was 1.9333, the settle 2.05). `reloadempty` (magazine in the gun) is untouched and still hits the bolt release.
  - `breathing`, `held_at`, `BREATH_*`: applied in `build()` to every clip whose name starts with `idle`. `main` (parent of the model, both arms and the sights in all 20 kinds) rises and falls once per 3.0 s loop, +-0.4 units at a pistol's distance from the eye (1.9 blocks) and in proportion for a kind held nearer, so the size on screen is the same for every kind (M9 0.40, M16 0.24, pump shotgun 0.11, magazines 0.36 units); sideways +-0.1 units at twice the rate; pitch +-0.4 and roll +-0.3 degrees. 25 linear keys per channel (the viewer and the game draw the same curve); first key = last key = the rest pose.
- `tools/vic-guns/geoview.py`: `FOV` (70), `first_person_matrix` also takes the base model as an object. No change to what it renders.
- `fabric-mod/src/main/java/pzcraft/mc/VicGunItem.java` (`adjustModelBonesForRender`): in first person, while an idle clip is the current one (`lastClip` empty or `idle*`), the `main` snapshot's translation and rotation are multiplied by `1 - aim`. A shot's kick is not touched.
- New `tools/vic-guns/bbexport.py <kind> [--set DIR]`: writes `tools/blockbench-models/<kind>.bbmodel` (GeckoLib format, model + embedded texture + every clip, bones the game hides not shown), following Blockbench's own Bedrock import rules. `tools/blockbench-models` is the root of the `blockbench-headless` MCP server (`.mcp.json`). `build.py` stays the source: an edit made in Blockbench has to be carried back by hand. The projects embed Point Blank's models: keep them private.

Changed clips: `idle*` in every kind; `reloademptynomag` in `m16a1`; `draw`, `load`, `unloadround` in the four magazines. Every other clip, every model and every texture is byte-identical. New manifest `cb3a4f2c…682b4c` (scratch; reproduce with `python tools/vic-guns/build.py --out <dir outside the project>`).

### 2026-10-14 19:30 CST — release fixes: start-up guidance, freeze while paused (Claude, VPS; built, NOT run)

Owner: "for now i need to fix some things for release mod. i suddenly got a competition. * easeness of usage ... while at main menu of pz an warning text showing load minecraft. so the process of play Start PZ -> Start Minecraft -> Start PZ World Create/Load -> Press O to start * minecraft hands and animations were running while pz is paused and paused menu is active".
**1. Start-up guidance (`pz-mod`: new `StartupHint`, `Patch_StartupHint` on `zombie.ui.UIManager.render` @OnExit):** a box top centre in PZ's own fonts (TextManager Large/Medium, black backdrop 0.65). Main menu (`GameWindow.states.current` is `MainScreenState`): amber "PzCraft: start Minecraft now / Launch Minecraft with the PzCraft mod and wait for it to load, / then create or load a world here and press O." until `LinkService.connected()`, then green "PzCraft: Minecraft is connected / Now create or load a world, then press O to play as Steve." In game (`IngameState`): "PzCraft needs the Viewpoint mod" if Viewpoint is missing; "Minecraft is not running" while not connected; green "press O to play as Steve" until the 3D view has been on once this session. Any exception hides it for the session (never thrown into PZ). API checked with `javap` against the VPS copy of `projectzomboid.jar` (MainScreenState.render ends its UI pass with UIManager.render; TextManager.DrawStringCentre/MeasureStringX/getFontHeight; UIManager.DrawTexture/getBlack).
**2. Freeze while paused (`pz-mod`: new `PauseFreeze`):** "paused" = `GameTime.isGamePaused()` (SP: speed 0) **or** PZ's in-game Esc menu visible (Lua `MainScreen.instance.javaObject.isVisible()` while in `IngameState`), worked out once a frame on the game thread (`FrameTick` → `PauseFreeze.update()`, Lua is only read there) into a volatile. While paused and no Minecraft screen owns the input: `Overlay` keeps drawing the last uploaded picture with the camera meta it was rendered through (no new frame, no `readMeta`), `NativeEntities.beginFrame` keeps the last entity/hand frame. Root cause on the MC side: Minecraft already pauses on PZ's PAUSED bit, but its gun sway/breathing (`GunFeel`), recoil, shells and mesh clips run on `System.nanoTime()`, and Minecraft keeps rendering; PZ also draws the hands itself from that stream. The PAUSED bit sent to Minecraft (`InputForwarder`), forwarded input (`live`), the gun keys R/I/B/X (`GuiInput`) and gun sync (`GunBridge`) now use the same signal, so the Esc menu pauses Minecraft too even if PZ's speed is not 0. Control API `/state` adds `pzPaused`, `ingameMenu`.
**Built:** `pz-mod` `gradlew build` BUILD SUCCESSFUL → `build/libs/PzCraft.jar` (classes `StartupHint`, `Patch_StartupHint`, `PauseFreeze` present). Fabric unchanged by this. README "Start it": the player flow and the pause behaviour.
**Not verified:** nothing run (the VPS cannot run PZ). To check at maple: the box on the main menu before/after Minecraft connects, its place against PZ's menu art at the owner's resolution, the in-game "press O" box, Esc menu and Space pause both freeze hands/guns/HUD and Minecraft (`pzctl state` → `pzPaused`, `ingameMenu`), unpausing resumes, an MC chest screen still works. If the Esc menu turns out to set speed 0 itself, the Lua check is just redundant.
**Open for the owner:** what goes into the competition release (this workspace has unreleased work: 0.15 weapons, M1A/JS-14/Trapper own models, inventory mirror; public `main` is v0.13.1 + discontinued note), its version number, and the public README's start steps.
**Release staging (19:45; owner: scope "Decide after testing at maple", version "0.15.0"):** new `tools/package-release.py <version>` builds `fabric-mod` with `-Pversion=<version>` (dev builds keep 0.13.0, which the dev scripts name) and `pz-mod`, and stages into git-ignored `release-staging/<version>/`: `Project-ZombCraft-Fabric-0.15.0.jar` 472176 B `78d528a623108d900a05afff5b67acdec11c476e0fc8c9e0b795cf88f3d83b68` (fabric.mod.json version 0.15.0), `Project-ZombCraft-Zomboid-0.15.0.zip` 345743 B `d6f192cdbeb27c9aa0dc755e4004f3c4f932a5f6cef6471ca8979403026c777c` (`PzCraft/common/`, `PzCraft/42/mod.info` modversion 0.15.0 and "(personal build)" dropped, `42/media/scripts/pzcraft_items.txt`, `42/media/java/PzCraft.jar` with the two fixes), `SHA256SUMS`. Built from this workspace as it is (all unreleased work included); nothing published, nothing committed. Minor: `pz-mod` `Main` still logs "PzCraft 0.13.0 loading". **Next:** owner tests at maple (the two fixes, the player flow with these files), then picks the release scope; publishing (public repo commit, tag, GitHub release) only on the owner's word.
**Draw order (20:10; owner: "minecraft is visible at paused screen. when it should've dimmed as pz paused screen is. probably fix is z-index", "the minecraft is visible at map mode", "i meant map mode pz ui. the minecraft draw is on top of it"):** cause: `Overlay` drew the Minecraft layer (world video, native hands, HUD) at `Display.update`, after everything incl. PZ's UI, so it sat over the Esc menu's dimming and over PZ's map UI. PZ's in-game order (`IngameState.renderInternal`, javap): world (`renderframe`; Viewpoint queues its `SceneDrawer` GenericDrawer from `IsoCell.render`) → `Core.RenderOffScreenBuffer()` (world to screen) → `renderframetext` → `renderframeui` (UIManager: Esc menu, map, windows, our StartupHint). **Fix:** new `Patch_WorldOnScreen` (`Core.RenderOffScreenBuffer` @OnExit) → `Overlay.queue()` queues the layer as a `TextureDraw.GenericDrawer` (`SpriteRenderer.drawGeneric`, as Viewpoint does) in `IngameState` with the 3D view on and Minecraft ready; `Display.update` no longer draws it (only `NativeBlocks.notePresented` + `Control.afterFrame` screenshots stay there). On a frame PZ does not draw the world the layer is not drawn, so it can never lie over a full-screen UI. PZ build BUILD SUCCESSFUL. **Not run.** Check at maple: layer still visible in play (if it vanishes, PZ's UI pass overwrites the screen after the world blit: then queue it at the start of the UI pass instead), Esc menu dims it, map covers it, MC chest screen still shows (it is in the HUD layer, now under PZ's UI: PZ's own windows are hidden while Steve drives), screenshots (`pzctl shot`) still include it.
**Re-staged (20:15) with the draw-order fix:** `Project-ZombCraft-Zomboid-0.15.0.zip` 347001 B `b6da7338b7c51033cf87ef680681a8af353d57705b899508a1bf0986823c387f` (supersedes `d6f192cd…`); the Fabric jar is unchanged (`78d528a6…`).

### 2026-10-14 20:50 CST — one Minecraft world per PZ save (Claude, VPS; built, NOT run)

Owner: "another feature to add that multiple worlds each it own minecraft world. maybe this is already implemented. pzworld1 has mcworld1, pzworld2 has mcworld2". It was not: `WorldBootstrap` opened one hidden world `pzcraft` at Minecraft's title screen for every save. Owner's choice for that world: **"First save loaded keeps it"**.
**Protocol:** `Wire.MSG_PZ_WORLD = 74` (PZ -> MC, "<Core.gameMode>/<Core.gameSaveWorld>" while `GameLoadingState`/`IngameState`, "" otherwise, ~1/s) and `MSG_MC_WORLD = 75` (MC -> PZ, the save whose world is open, "" none, ~1/s).
**PZ (`pz-mod`):** new `WorldIdentity` (sends the key from `FrameTick`; `minecraftHasThisSave()` = Minecraft's reported save equals ours, true if Minecraft never reports); `LinkService` keeps `minecraftWorld` (null on disconnect); `PuppetDriver` does not place Steve until Minecraft has this save's world (so pressing O during a switch cannot put him in the old save's world); `StartupHint` in game: amber "Minecraft is opening this save's world / Wait a moment, then press O." while it differs.
**Minecraft (`fabric-mod`):** `WorldBootstrap` rewritten: with a save key from PZ it opens/creates that save's level from the title screen (display name "PzCraft <save>"); if another level is open it `disconnectFromWorld` (Minecraft's save-and-quit-to-title) first, then opens; collision/material/coverage caches are reset before opening. Mapping `saves/pzcraft-worlds.properties` (`<mode>/<save>=<folder>`): first unmapped save takes `pzcraft` if it exists and no save has it, others get `pzcraft-<slug>-<sha1[0:8]>`. With PZ's main menu ("") nothing is opened and an open world stays. Fallback: PZ connected 15 s without ever reporting (older PZ jar, the standalone test harness) → the shared `pzcraft` as before. `Session.pzWorld/mcWorld`; `PzCraftClient` handles MSG_PZ_WORLD and reports MSG_MC_WORLD from the link loop.

### 2026-10-15 11:16 CST — Viewpoint 0.1.6 sync: hooks ported (Claude, VPS; built and staged, NOT run in game)

Owner suggested steamcmd for the jar. **steamcmd on the VPS, no root:** `~/.local/steamcmd/run.sh` (Valve's steamcmd run through a user-local 32-bit C runtime unpacked from Ubuntu's `libc6-i386`, `lib32gcc-s1`, `lib32stdc++6`; its self-updates live in `~/.local/steamcmd/rt/usr`; remove the folder plus `~/Steam` to uninstall). `run.sh +login anonymous +workshop_download_item 108600 3809306528 validate +quit` downloaded Viewpoint **0.1.6** (`mod.info` modversion 0.1.6, `Viewpoint.jar` 33,700,592 B) to `~/Steam/steamapps/workshop/content/108600/3809306528`; copy in git-ignored `.pz-jars/viewpoint-0.1.6/` with its classes decompiled to `.pz-jars/viewpoint-0.1.6/src` (vineflower) for interoperability only. **Licence note:** Viewpoint's `LICENSE` is "proprietary and confidential ... may not be redistributed, re-uploaded, repackaged, decompiled for reuse or modified"; nothing of it goes into PzCraft, and the new copy stays out of git (the older decompiled `ref/viewpoint-src` IS tracked in the private repo: the owner may want to drop it).
**Audit:** every Viewpoint class/member PzCraft names (patches and reflection) checked with `javap` against 0.1.6; a new script check (scratchpad `patchcheck.py`) resolves all ZombieBuddy `@Patch` targets in `pz-mod` against the Viewpoint and PZ jars, including `@Argument` indices: **55 patches, 0 problems** after the port. The tester's 2026-10-14 log (already 0.1.6) also shows the native-block shader still links with Viewpoint's light library.
**Changed (`pz-mod`):**
- `Patch_SteadyEye` → `viewpoint.input.Eye.place` (was `Controls.eye`, gone; same arguments and steady-state list) — Steve's eye height/position follows Minecraft's again.
- `Patch_SteveThirdPerson` → `viewpoint.camera.ThirdPerson.poll` (class moved; it now also handles shoulder swap and camera-behind keys, all skipped while Steve drives).
- `ViewpointBridge` third-person flag → `viewpoint.core.View.thirdPerson` (was `input.ThirdPerson.active`): forcing third person off while Steve drives and restoring it works again.
- `SteveCamera.draw` also resets SceneDrawer's new `up` vector: 0.1.6 rolls the view with its camera shake (`CameraView.direction` sets dir and up on every first-person frame), which would tilt PZ's world under Minecraft's picture.
- New `Patch_SteveCameraStep` (`ThirdPerson.capture` @OnExit → `SteveCamera.unkick`): 0.1.6's first-person kicks (a shot, a blow via `IsoZombie.Hit`, an explosion nearby) also zoom the projection; while Steve drives it is set back to `View.fovY(false)` (which keeps PzCraft's ADS zoom) and `View.frameFovY`.
- New `Patch_StevePanini` (`Panini.strength` → 0 while Steve drives; Panini is off by default, but bends PZ's world against Minecraft's picture if a player turns it on).
- New `Patch_SteveFreeLook` (`FreeLook.update` skipped while Steve drives: Mouse 4 would snap the view back on release).
- Comments in `SteveEye` name the new classes.
- **Considered and left as is:** middle click still toggles Viewpoint's cursor in Steve mode (it did before 0.1.6, README documents it). Viewpoint's new `CombatManager` shot hooks do not touch Steve's shots (`GunBridge` rolls PZ's hit chance and calls `Hit` directly). The new hit pause (`HitStop` via `getAnimationTimeDelta`) briefly slows a zombie Steve hits, as Viewpoint intends. Not checked offline: Viewpoint's experimental FSR frame generation queues its own drawer at the same `Core.RenderOffScreenBuffer` exit where PzCraft queues the Minecraft layer (`Patch_WorldOnScreen`); with frame generation on, the order of the two decides whether generated frames include the Minecraft layer.
**Build:** `pz-mod` BUILD SUCCESSFUL; `package-release.py 0.15.0` → Fabric `aa6f11e3…59ff` (unchanged, byte-identical), Zomboid zip `0c984f3affdfb2aa1f17e27c8b4093c97cc1f24f43ba7099e0f9cb285d72e913` (355,110 B; supersedes `b946de79…`), new patch classes inside. **Not run in game.**
**Next (maple, with 0.1.6):** console must show `patching viewpoint.input.Eye.place`, `viewpoint.camera.ThirdPerson.poll`/`capture`, `viewpoint.render.Panini.strength`, `viewpoint.input.FreeLook.update` and no `[!] Method ... not found`; Steve's eye height (stand, sneak) matches Minecraft; shoot/hit zombies and stand near an explosion: PZ's world stays locked to Minecraft's picture (no roll, no zoom); Shift+O does nothing while Steve drives and Viewpoint's third-person setting comes back after O off; Panini on in Viewpoint's settings → no bending while Steve drives; Mouse 4 does nothing; frame generation on → Minecraft layer still visible and steady.

### 2026-10-15 11:58 CST — Easier start: PZ starts Minecraft, Mojang loading screen, Steve or Zomboid survivor (Claude, VPS; built and staged, NOT run in game)

Owner's design (approved plan `~/.claude/plans/cheeky-wiggling-anchor.md` on the VPS): PZ's menu waits for and starts Minecraft; a Mojang loading screen while it loads; before character creation the player picks a PZ character or Steve; PZ character = Viewpoint first person only, never Steve, PZ creation as usual; Steve = no PZ creation, straight to the spawn, no PZ items; changing kind = a new character; Viewpoint-like UI as a placeholder; PZ starts Minecraft, players may start it themselves; old saves need no support.
**Reference added on the VPS:** PZ 42.21's Lua from the dedicated server (`~/pz-dedicated`, steamcmd app 380870, 6.9 GB; its `zombie/core/Core.class` equals our 42.21 jar's). Viewpoint draws its windows with Dear ImGui (PZ's bundled imgui-java 1.86, own context, hook `@OnExit Display.imguiEndFrame`); Viewpoint 0.1.6 refuses first person until its setup wizard is finished (`viewpoint.platform.Onboarding.blocks()`).
**Built:**

### 2026-10-15 13:39 CST — Multiplayer design confirmed: host runs both; PZMC's multiplayer studied (Claude, VPS; plan only so far)

Owner: "check their multiplayer code and not exactly copy 1:1 but we can copy how it works onto ours"; after the comparison ("which is easier in long run and both can have minecraft mods?": host-runs-both = long-run easier for anything Minecraft and full mod support; PZ-server-only = faster, partial mod support) the owner chose **host runs both** (as on 2026-10-14). Approved plan (M0 foundation + M1 move together; M2-M5 later) on the VPS at `~/.claude/plans/cheeky-wiggling-anchor.md`.
**PZMC's multiplayer (studied, GPL: ideas only):** Lua server half `PZMC_Server.lua` on `Events.OnClientCommand`; each player's Java asks with `GameClient.instance.sendClientCommand(player, "PZMC", cmd, KahluaTable)` at one choke point per kind of change (single player: apply; MP: ask; same request within 2 s once); the server checks (own item types, near the player within 12 tiles), applies as PZ's own server-side actions do (`AddWorldInventoryItem` + `transmitCompleteItemToClients`, `sendAddItemToContainer`, `zombie:setHealth`/`Kill(player)` by online id), and answers/relays with `sendServerCommand` (poses to players within 90 tiles, 15/s). Their blocks are PZ world items with a place key in mod data, so PZ's own server saves and syncs them. Lessons we take: zero `setLastFallSpeed`/`setFallTime` on the server every tick for players whose falls are Minecraft's; XP, eating and health only count on the server; `AntiCheatSpeed=4` or hold speed at 17 tiles/s; each game tells the server whether its player is Steve so the others hide/draw the right body; trust is clients' (role checks only).
**Our design:** player pair (PZ client ↔ MC client, as today; MC client joins the host's MC server) + host pair (PZ server ↔ MC server on a second link `pzcraft-host`): collision around every player, zombies, hits, world edits, time/weather, mirror. Our Java must run in the PZ server process (ZombieBuddy). A Lua server half `PzCraft_Server.lua` takes the requests that start in a player's PZ game ("steve" with the Minecraft name, "falls", later containers/items).
