### 2026-10-15 15:15 CST — Multiplayer M1 (move together) in source; server halves verified headless on the VPS (Claude, VPS)

**PZ side (pz-mod):**
- `McLink` (new): one interface for PZ's links to a Minecraft. `LinkService.LINK` is the player link; `HostLink.LINK` is the host link (TX lock, epoch bumped when the Minecraft server (re)connects).
- `WorldExporter`: now one instance per link, following several players (a `Mover` per player with its own velocity lead). The wanted columns are the union around all players, the queue is sorted by the nearest player, and a section is retracted only when no player is near it. The static API is unchanged for single player (`tick(player)`, `readyAt`, `stats`, …). In a PZ server, `startHost`/`hostTick(GameServer.getPlayers())` feed the Minecraft server.
- `HostLink.tick()` runs on PZ's game thread from Lua `Events.OnTickEvenPaused` (via `PzCraft.Server.tick()` in `ServerSettings`). Plain `OnTick` does not fire while an empty server is paused (`PauseEmpty`). It runs the exporter for every online player, `ClockBridge.serverTick`, then `WeatherBridge.serverTick`; errors are caught and it stops after 10. The link thread handles `MSG_CLOCK_CHANGE`, `MSG_WEATHER_COMMAND` and ignores `MSG_CLOCK_CONTROL` (PZ's server keeps its day length).
- `ClockBridge`: a server tick whose offset is kept in `GameTime` mod data, so a Minecraft `/time set` or a night slept through moves PZ's clock for everyone.
- `WeatherBridge`: a per-link tick; on the server, Minecraft's `/weather` commands become PZ's admin weather.
- Multiplayer guards lifted in `SteveControl`, `PuppetDriver` and `HealthLink`. `HealthLink` stays inactive in MP in practice, because no `MSG_STEVE_HEALTH` comes from a Minecraft server yet (M2).
- Kept off on MP clients until M4: `MirrorBridge` (status line "off: multiplayer") and `ContainerBridge`. `PauseFreeze` never pauses in MP.
- Remote Steves: `FrameTick.hidesOwnModel` hides the PZ body of another player found in `MultiplayerLua.playsSteve`.
- `MultiplayerLua` gained `drivesSteve()` and `minecraftName()`. Client Lua sends "falls" {ours} and "steve" {on, mc} whenever this changes (checked every 10 ticks).
**Minecraft side (fabric-mod):**
- `PzCraftServer` applies host-link messages through the same code as the client: collision, collision clear, materials, terrain column, clock state, weather state. It resets the fields when the PZ server (re)connects, drains `Session`'s outbox, and registers `WorldRules` and `TimeBridge.tick` (END_SERVER_TICK) plus `WeatherBridge.init`. At start it warns when the world is not the empty flat world.
- `-Dpzcraft.audit=true` runs the mixin audit and loads and initialises every PzCraft class, logging those that need the client.
- `WorldRules` (new): single player's world rules, out of `ActorProxies.configureWorld`.
- `ServerPlacement` (new): payload `pzcraft:place`, serverbound, registered in the common init. The server teleports the asking player, at most once a second, and keeps an "awaiting" set from join and respawn. The client asks for placement when it has no integrated server, is released only once it is within 2 blocks of the target, and asks again every 40 ticks.
- `ServerFloatingMixin` (common, `noBlocksAround`): PZ collision counts as ground, and a player awaiting placement is never "floating". A dedicated server would otherwise kick Steve after 80 ticks; the integrated server always allows flight.
- `RespawnBridge.prepare`: single player only. On a dedicated server it would have held every respawn waiting for a PZ acknowledgement that never comes.
- `MirrorBridge.tick` (MC): single player only. It took `getPlayers().getFirst()`.
**Docs:** `release/multiplayer-test.md` (new host and players guide: `AntiCheatSpeed=4`, the empty flat world settings, ports, the log lines to expect); README known-limits line; changelog draft line (MP in testing instead of "stays off").
**Evidence (VPS, scratch servers, no players):**
- Builds: PZ `c240d8ef6aef91fc26ef109ab5da4aa7f3a181de8ee17453af62c1f95934f9cc`, Fabric `e06b5e563518c0a63e124c1bf1c4365ae08296717fa2a87508fb521439f3ca49`. Lua parses (luaparser). `patchcheck.py`: 0 problems.
- PZ server (ZB console approval of the matching SHA-256, once, not saved) and Fabric server paired. The PZ tick runs (`Minecraft server (re)connected: resending collision` from the exporter).
- Minecraft → PZ: `/weather rain` produced PZ `weather from Minecraft: rain for 22.144 h`. `/time set 6000` produced PZ `Minecraft changed PZ clock to 1993-07-09 12.0 (ticks=6000)`.
- PZ → Minecraft: `PZ weather: precipitation 0.5 … -> Minecraft rain=true`. Minecraft's day clock follows PZ's frozen (paused, empty) clock: 6062 → 6122 → 6022 at 10 s steps (snapped back after drifting over 150).
- PZ server restart with Minecraft running: Minecraft logged disconnected and connected again, and the exporter resent.
- World check: a default flat world warned. A new world made with `level-type=minecraft:flat` plus air layers started with no warning. The first version crashed the server with an NPE (air layers come back as null from `getLayers()`); fixed with `getLayersInfo()` and a try/catch.
- Audit: 76 classes load on a server and 16 need the client. None of the 16 is called from server-reachable code (callers are client classes, client mixins, or `ChunkBlockMixin` behind `Session.CLIENT`).
- Gotcha: a Minecraft dedicated server stops ticking after `pause-when-empty-seconds` (60) without players. Commands still run, but END_SERVER_TICK work (clock and weather) waits for a player; the scratch server is set to -1 for tests.
**Not verified (needs PCs, 2 players):** joining (placement packet, no flying kick, no "moved wrongly" churn), moving together, each other's Steve visible and PZ bodies hidden, falls, AntiCheatSpeed, collision around a second player on the server. Known gaps for later steps are in the guide (M2 zombies, M3 world edits, M4 items, M5 choice/hosting). The trust note: the server honours any player's placement request (PZMC trusts clients too); checking it against the PZ server's position for that player waits for the identity map.
**Next:** owner/maple: a two-PC test per `release/multiplayer-test.md`, or start M2's short plan. Re-stage 0.16.0 when the owner decides whether multiplayer ships in it.

### 2026-10-15 15:47 CST — our own M9 Pistol, first pass (Claude, VPS; source and offline preview only)

Owner asked whether Point Blank's M9, M1911, B-F and M16 could simply be recoloured and reshaped to fit PZ. Answer: an edited copy is still Vic's work (All Rights Reserved) and can't ship without his OK, so we make our own, sized from PZ's own meshes. Owner: "Yes, build our own M9 from PZ's mesh. Offline preview first, then the M1911, B-F and M16." This restarts gun work (paused at 10:18) for these four guns only.

**Made (all numbers new; nothing read from the Point Blank jar):**
- `tools/vic-guns/pzmesh.py` (new): reads a PZ `.x` gun mesh from the VPS's dedicated-server install (`~/pz-dedicated/media/models_X/weapons/firearm/`; no textures there), turns it to the .geo.json's axes and scale, and draws side/top/back outlines on a unit grid plus a side profile as text. Reference only: PZ's meshes are never bundled. M9 at `--length 29.5 --muzzle -20 --top 25.5`: 19.5 high, 3.9 wide; sights 25.35, slide top 24.6 front / 24.9 back, guard bottom 14.1, grip bottom 6.0, grip lean about 13.4°.
- `pistol.py` (new): our pistol joint layout, solved from each pistol's numbers: main, gun, slide, hammer, magazine/bullet and magazine2/bullet2 leaning with the grip, the arms, scope (EYE 19 behind the rear sight), muzzleflash, _shelleject_. An arm gets the least turn that points it from a chosen shoulder at the middle of the hand; its 8x24x8 box ends PALM past that point. The first-person placement was tuned by rendering. Third person uses the mod's common size and offset (the same as `rifle_skeleton.json`). `feed_arm` solves the held magazine's left arm so it comes in from the left while feeding. `free_patch` finds texture space for the round texels.
- `m9.py` (new): 82 cubes on a 256² texture, black slide, frame and grips at PZ's widths:
  - slide: the open top over the barrel, the nose with the front sight, the side walls, the right-side port, a rear block with a sloped back, a dovetail rear sight, three white dots, decocker levers, the extractor, serrations painted into the texture;
  - barrel with bore and chamber block;
  - frame: dust cover, guard, trigger, takedown lever and button, slide stop, magazine catch;
  - grip leaning 13.4° with checkered panels and a medallion; the tang; a cocked spur hammer;
  - magazine: body, lips, a level base plate and the top round.
- `pistol_anim.py` and `m9_anim.py` (new): our own clips, built with m1a_anim's rig:
  - `idle`/`idleempty`: empty means the slide locked at 4.6 and no top rounds;
  - `draw`/`drawempty`;
  - `fire`: the slide goes 5.0 back and home in .065 s, the hammer is struck and recocked, a kick;
  - `reload` 1.75 s: the gun turns in, the magazine drops 3.5, the left hand strips it away, lines the new one up 4.5 under the grip and pushes it home at 1.12;
  - `reloadempty` 2.05 s: the same, then an overhand rack off the slide stop;
  - `rack` 1.0 s: overhand, from a shoulder out to the left and forward so the forearm doesn't cover the view;
  - `inspect`/`inspectempty` 3.2 s: the left hand away, the left side shown, then the right;
  - `running`, `runningstart`, `runningend`.
  
  Sound marks `m9_unload`/`reload`/`action`/`rest`/`draw`. DATA: reload 1350 ms, reloadempty 1750 ms, `viewRecoilAmplitude` 3.0 (the current M9's feel, unchanged).
- `build_own.py`:
  - a per-kind layout: `model.skeleton()` when the module has one, else `rifle_skeleton.json`;
  - kind `m9` (`Base.Pistol`, pistol, pistol shell);
  - the held magazine `mag9mm` (`Base.9mmClip`), made from our M9 by build.py's `magazine_item_geo`/`recipe_feed` with `m9.magazine_spec`;
  - recipes get the name `own_<kind>`, so build.py's Point Blank tables (`HAND_RACKED` has `m9`, `CHARGED` has `m16a1`) never touch our guns.
- `m1a.paint`: `decor` can be a dict {material: pattern}. A plain function still means wood only.
- Tooling note: the VPS system Python has neither numpy nor Pillow. A scratch venv was used.

### 2026-10-15 14:00 CST — PZ closes at startup: fixed and checked up to the main menu (Claude, maple-pc; built and installed; PZ run, Minecraft not started)

Owner: "PZ closes at startup, before the menu" (after the 13:37 changes). Reproduced with `scripts\run-pz.bat` alone (PZ only, no Minecraft started; dev policy `PZ_ZB_ARGS=policy=allow-all,config_dir=…\.pzcraft\zb-dev` for these launches so no approval dialog blocked them).

**Evidence (before the fix):**
- `pz-launch.log` (appended by run-pz.bat), run at 21:45:50–21:46:13: `FATAL ERROR in native method: Thread[#80,MainThread,5,Main]: No context is current or a function that is not available in the current context was called. The JVM will abort execution.` Stack: `org.lwjgl.opengl.GL20C.glCreateShader` ← `imgui.gl3.ImGuiImplGl3.createAndCompileShader` ← `createShaders` ← `createDeviceObjects` ← `init(ImGuiImplGl3.java:215)` ← `pzcraft.pz.PzUi.create(PzUi.java:114)` ← `PzUi.frameEnd(PzUi.java:82)` ← `org.lwjglx.opengl.Display.imguiEndFrame(Display.java:781)` ← `zombie.core.Core.EndFrameUI(Core.java:959)` ← `zombie.gameStates.MainScreenState.render(MainScreenState.java:578)`.
- The same abort was the 21:38 session's exit: its DebugLog stops at `binkId: 2` (21:39:56) with no `GameThread exited` line, and no Windows application error or hs_err file (the JVM abort writes to pz-launch.log, not to the DebugLog).
- Why only now: PzUi's start screen is needed only when Minecraft is not connected (`StartScreen.shown()`), so runs that already had Minecraft connected (21:16 Prism session) never reached `PzUi.create`. Viewpoint draws its ImGui the same way but from `Display.update` (`ref/viewpoint-src/viewpoint/Patch_FrameSwap.java`), which runs on the render thread that owns the context. `ref/pz-src/zombie/MainThread.java` runs the main loop on its own thread (`mainLoop`, `Thread.yield()` at line 73); PZ's render context is not current there.

**Changed (source):**
- `pz-mod/src/main/java/pzcraft/pz/Patch_PzUiFrame.java`: `@Patch(className = "org.lwjglx.opengl.Display", methodName = "update")` with `@OnEnter enter(boolean processMessages) → PzUi.frameEnd()` (was `imguiEndFrame` `@OnExit`). Javadoc updated.
- `pz-mod/src/main/java/pzcraft/pz/PzUi.java`: `glReady()` (GLFW current context non-zero, `GL.getCapabilities()` with `glCreateShader` and `glDrawElements` loaded), called in `frameEnd()` after the screen check; class and `frameEnd` javadoc updated; imports `org.lwjgl.opengl.GL`, `GLCapabilities`.

**Build:** `.\pz-mod\gradlew.bat -p pz-mod build --console=plain` → BUILD SUCCESSFUL (with `glReady` alone and then with the hook move). Jar sha256 `441367175e6be8cfb6894ded6acaeb2739da7538fe360473b89664d60944dcd0`, 411336 B.

**Installed:** yes, `gradlew installMod` → `E:\Zomboid\mods\PzCraft\42\media\java\PzCraft.jar` = the same hash. The previous installed jar (`948b0a60…`, 411064 B, 21:35) and the glReady-only jar (`de87ba63…`, 411309 B, 21:48) are overwritten; both are rebuildable from this tree.

**Checks after the fix (PZ run via `scripts\run-pz.bat`, dev policy, Minecraft NOT started):**
- Launched 21:56:45: PZ still running at 21:59:16 (`pz-launch.log`: 0 new FATAL lines). DebugLog `2026-10-15_21-56_DebugLog.txt`: `[PzCraft] PzCraft screens: ImGui ready.` at 21:57:10.
- Window capture of PZ (PrintWindow, no focus change): the main menu with PzCraft's start screen: "Start Minecraft", "Project ZombCraft (Prism Launcher)" with **Play**, "I'll start it myself", "Play without Minecraft". ### 2026-10-15 23:10 CST — multiplayer: player 1 in the MP world; two mod bugs found; fixes built, NOT installed (game running) (Claude, maple-pc)

Owner: "Connected". The PZ client is `IngameState` (`inGame: true`, control `/state`), `link.connected: true`, `ready: false`. The Minecraft client has NOT joined yet (no `joining the multiplayer Minecraft server` line; its log stops at 22:42).

**Bugs found in the logs:**
1. **Client, every frame: `IsoWorld.renderInternal> Exception thrown` — `IllegalAccessError: failed to access class pzcraft.pz.CharacterType from class viewpoint.camera.ThirdPerson`.** `Patch_SteveThirdPerson` (`@Patch viewpoint.camera.ThirdPerson.poll`, `@OnEnter(skipOn = true)`) calls `CharacterType.locksView()`; ZombieBuddy inlines the advice into Viewpoint's class, and `CharacterType` / `locksView()` were package-private. This is in 0.16.0 too (CharacterType is new in 0.16), so it would also break single-player world rendering on every frame. Fixed in source: `public final class CharacterType`, `public static boolean locksView()` (javap: public in the built jar). Possibly what the owner saw as "unplayable" with 0.16.0 (not verified: the 0.16.0 world was never rendered in a log).
2. **Server, on every "falls" command: `Object tried to call nil in onClientCommand` at `PzCraft_Server.lua:54`** — `next(ownFalls)` is nil in PZ's Lua. Because "falls" comes in the same command batch as "hello", the server's `hello` reply may not have arrived either: the client has no `multiplayer: this server's Minecraft server is 127.0.0.1:25565` line, so the Minecraft client was never told where to join. Fixed in source: the loop `for _ in pairs(ownFalls) do anyOwnFalls = true break end`.

**Built (not installed):** `pz-mod\build\libs\PzCraft.jar` 411336 B, sha256 `5f20f78eef7adb1c0a32c798f9fb670f157aebe2ae634b300112f7e095f3b820` (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`). The installed jar in `E:\Zomboid\mods\PzCraft` is still `441367175e…` (the running PZ client has it open; `run-pz.bat` installs only while PZ is stopped). The MP server's Lua (`C:\Users\Maple\.pzcraft\mp\pz-server-cache\mods\PzCraft\42\media\lua\server\PzCraft_Server.lua`) and jar are also the old ones.

**To apply (needs the owner's go-ahead: both restarts disconnect the player):** stop the PZ client (PID 2644) and the PZ server (PID 17128) → copy the new `E:\Zomboid\mods\PzCraft` (after `gradlew installMod`) into the server cache `mods\PzCraft` → start `scripts\mp\run-pz-server.bat` → start `scripts\run-pz.bat` (dev policy) → join again (`serverConnect` via `/lua`, or the MULTIPLAYER screen) → spawn NEXT → check: `multiplayer: this server's Minecraft server is 127.0.0.1:25565` in the client log, `PzCraft_Server.lua` errors gone, the Minecraft client joins (`joining the multiplayer Minecraft server 127.0.0.1:25565`), the MC server log `put at their PZ character` for the player.

### 2026-10-16 12:27 CST — W0 + W2a: mining PZ grass, bushes, stones, twigs, logs, boulders and ores (Claude, VPS; built and checked, not run in game)

**How it works.** PZ's `MineBridge` sends, every 200 ms when it changes and every second regardless, the things within 6 tiles (levels z-1..z+1) Minecraft can mine (`MSG_TARGETS` 78, `WorldTargets`: kind, variant, tile, level, sprite-name hash, a box). Minecraft's crosshair (`PzMiningPickMixin` on `LocalPlayer.raycastHitResult`) takes the nearest one as a block hit when nothing nearer is hit; a zombie under the crosshair always wins over grass, bushes and stones (attack through them), and over a boulder when nearer. Holding attack (`PzMiningAttackMixin` on `Minecraft.startAttack`/`continueAttack`) runs vanilla's mining sum against a stand-in block (`PzMineables`: short grass, oak leaves, cobblestone, oak log, calcite, stone, iron ore, copper ore) with the thing's own hardness, plays the stand-in's hit and break sounds and crack particles, and draws a thin bar under the crosshair; 5 ticks between things broken by holding. Done: the target is hidden on the client for up to 2 s and `pzcraft:mine_pz` goes to the server (`PzMineServer`), which checks that PZ still reports it and that it is within reach + 2, wears the tool (`ItemStack.mineBlock` on the stand-in), and sends `MSG_PZ_MINE` (79). PZ removes it as its own actions do (`transmitRemoveItemFromSquare`) with their zombie noise (radius 10; 20 for rock), re-exports the column and answers `MSG_PZ_MINED` (80). **Only a confirmed removal drops anything** (no duplication when PZ cannot remove it); unanswered requests expire after 200 ticks. Single player only: a dedicated Minecraft server has no targets (multiplayer M3).

**What counts (PZ's own rules, checked against all 33,568 tile definitions on the VPS, `~/pz-dedicated/media/*.tiles.txt`):**
- *Grass* (one target per tile, all removed together as PZ's "Remove grass"): `canBeRemoved`, not `attachedFloor` (that excludes street cracks and the flat grass overlays), not `IsMoveAble` (hedges), not `f_wallvines_*`. 179 tiles: e_newgrass_1 60, d_generic_1 60 (debris), d_plants_1 40, vegetation_farm_01 19.
- *Bush* (one per tile): `canBeCut`, as PZ's "Remove bush": 32 tiles of f_bushes_1 (16 wild bushes, 16 low hedges). The 16 hedges without `canBeCut` wait for furniture (W2c).
- *Ground cover* (each object): PZ's `GroundCoverItems` CustomNames and "...Stones", not `IsMoveAble`: 49 tiles (d_generic_1 17, boulders_ 16 Stone2/LargeStone, crafting_ore 16 Limestone).
- *Boulder/ore* (each object): `isOres()` sprites that are single-tile and not `IsMoveAble`: 62 tiles (crafting_ore 34: iron and copper ore small/medium/large, FlintBoulder, LimestoneBoulder; boulders_ 28). The 4-tile "Boulder" grid is left alone. Target box = the tile's collision top when the boulder blocks the tile.

**Mining rules and drops (owner's defaults):** grass instant, no tool (seeds 1 in 8; shears: short grass); bush hardness 0.6, any tool (1-2 sticks; shears: a bush); stones 0.3 by hand (1 / "Stones" 2 / large 3 cobblestone; with twigs +1 stick), large stones 0.6, flat stone and limestone 1, twigs 2 sticks, branch 3 sticks (0.2, axe faster), log 1 oak log (1.0); boulders need a pickaxe (nothing without, as stone): plain hardness 3 → 4-8 cobblestone, iron/copper ore hardness 2/3/4 by size, needing a stone pickaxe (vanilla's iron/copper ore tier) → raw iron 1/2/3 or raw copper 2/4/6 + 2 cobblestone, flint → 1-4 flint + 2 cobblestone, limestone boulder (2) → 2-5 cobblestone. Food exhaustion 0.005 per thing, as per block.

**Better Combat (optional, in the owner's mods):** it swings a weapon instead of mining when the crosshair block is air. `BetterCombatMiningMixin` (`@Pseudo`, `require = 0`) makes its `isTargetingMineableBlock` see the stand-in block at the aimed voxel: stones, bushes and boulders are mined with a weapon in hand; grass keeps Better Combat's "swing through grass" (as vanilla grass with Better Combat). Read from Better Combat 3.2.2's bytecode (`AttackInteractor.onDoAttack`/`onHandleBlockBreaking`).

**Files.** protocol: `WorldTargets` (new; targets, `Mine`, `Mined`, PZ's cover and ore names), `Wire` ids 78-80, `WorldTargetsTest` (new, 6 tests). pz-mod: `MineBridge` (new), `FrameTick` (tick), `LinkService` (`MSG_PZ_MINE`), `Control` (`pzMining` stats). fabric-mod: `PzTargets`, `PzMineables`, `PzMineServer`, `PzMining` (new), mixins `PzMiningPickMixin`, `PzMiningAttackMixin`, `BetterCombatMiningMixin` (new, client list), `PzCraftMain` (`PzMineServer.init`), `PzCraftClient` (`PzMining.init`, messages 78 and 80, target reset on PZ disconnect), `WorldBootstrap` (target reset on world change).

**Evidence (VPS):**
- Builds: protocol tests **68, 0 failures, 0 errors** (62 before + 6 `WorldTargetsTest`); pz-mod OK, `PzCraft.jar` 425,360 B `155630ffe97a3458fab702cc9590054a5d6e897271ae0183c8e158f032ad9aa2`; fabric-mod OK, `pzcraft-fabric-0.13.0.jar` 973,616 B `613851b9823a17442fee3019112dd252bfeed404eedae3cb01461527e2457d97`. `tools/patchcheck.py --viewpoint … --pz .pz-jars/projectzomboid.jar`: access 56/0 problems, targets 56/0 problems.

Not testable offline: tool tiers and `ItemStack`s need tags that load with a world.
- Not checked on the VPS: `MineBridge`'s scan and removal on a live PZ world; the client mixins and Better Combat's mixin actually applying (no client can start here).

Then W1's list (12:07 entry).

### 2026-10-16 12:50 CST — W2c + W3: furniture mined into PZ's own item, and placed back (Claude, VPS; built and checked, not run in game)

**What.** A tile's furniture is now a target (`KIND_FURNITURE`): the topmost `IsMoveAble` object on the tile (an object on a counter comes off before the counter, as PZ wants), not doors, windows, walls, fences, floors, things hanging on walls (`attachedN/W/S/E`, `MoveType` `WallObject`/`Window`/`WindowObject`/`FloorTile`). Rugs (`MoveType=FloorRug`) are a flat box 0.06 high, like a carpet. Box from where it stands (PZ's render offset when on a table) to its top surface; a piece that blocks the tile is as tall and wide as its collision box. A solid object PZ cannot pick up (`KIND_SOLID`: solid/solidtrans, not `IsMoveAble`) breaks into blocks of its material (wall drops; needs the material's tool). Classifying all 33,568 tile definitions: furniture 4,063 tiles in 90 tilesets (seating 278, camping 254, office 198, crafted 171, counters 144, lights 126, shops 119, storage 107, medical 105, high tables 102, rugs 102, carpentry 100…); solid 1,109 (industry, crafted, farm accessories, gas station pumps, distillery…); `IsMoveAble` tiles left out by the wall/floor/window flags: 2,481.
**Pick-up (PZ).** `FurnitureBridge.pickUp` calls `PzCraftMoveables.pickUp` (new `pz-mod/mod/42/media/lua/client/PzCraft_Moveables.lua`) through `LuaHelpers.callLuaClass`: PZ's own `ISMoveableSpriteProps.fromObject(object):pickUpMoveable(character, square, true, true)`. Forced, because Minecraft's mining stands in for PZ's checks (weight, skill, tools) and its chance to break the piece (that roll only runs when not forced); a container's contents spill onto the floor first (owner's default; PZ would refuse a full one). The item is exactly PZ's (single tile `<module>.<sprite name>`, multi-tile parts "Name (i/n)" or one forced-single item) and goes to the hidden PZ inventory; parts PZ leaves on the floor are moved there too (PZ's own world-item removal, as its `placeMoveable` does). The inventory mirror hands them to Steve (`moveable` is in `ItemRules.single`, so they travel whole). Counted as removed only when the object is gone from the square.
**Minecraft knows the item.** Furniture types are made from one script per sprite and are in no catalogue, so the mirror would have rejected them. PZ now adds `mv` (the script it was made from, `item.getScriptItem().getFullName()`) and `dn` (PZ's display name, e.g. a chair's name) to an item's state for Moveables (`ContainerBridge.state`; neither is part of the identity key). `PzItemCatalog.borrow(type, mv)` makes and remembers an entry under the item's own type from that script; `PzItems.stack` uses it, shows `dn` as the name and keeps `mv` in the stack (generic furniture icon for now).
**Placing (W3).** Right-click a floor (a face pointing up) holding a PZ furniture item (`PzFurniture.use`, before doors and containers in `UseMixin`; once per click): `MSG_PLACE_FURNITURE` (81) with the PZ item id (`i`), tile, level and facing (its front looks back at Steve, PZ's N/E/S/W). PZ (`FurnitureBridge.tick`) finds the item in the inventory by id and calls `PzCraftMoveables.place`: the sprite for that facing from PZ's own `getFaces()`, then PZ's own `canPlaceMoveable` and `placeMoveable` with the item's original sprite, as PZ's placing cursor does after a rotation; the item leaves PZ's inventory and the mirror takes it from Steve. `MSG_FURNITURE_PLACED` (82) answers; a refusal shows "It does not fit there" over the hotbar.
**Rules and drops (Minecraft).** Furniture: the material's stand-in and hardness (wood: planks, 2; metal: iron block, 3; fabric: wool…), no tool needed, nothing dropped by Minecraft. Solid: the material's stand-in times 1.5, the material's tool, wall drops (e.g. metal 4 iron bars). Neither is "replaceable"; zombies are hit through furniture, not through solid objects.

### 2026-10-16 13:35 CST — W7: zombies break what the player built (Claude, VPS; built and checked, not run in game)

**Minecraft blocks (`ZombieSiege`, Minecraft server, single player).** PZ now flags each zombie it sends whose target is the player (`Wire.Actor.FLAG_CHASING` = 16, `IsoZombie.getTarget() == player`). Such a zombie that has not moved 0.25 blocks in two seconds and has a Minecraft block between it and Steve, right in front at legs or chest height (straight towards Steve or along one axis, 0.6-1 block ahead), thumps it every 1.5 s. A Minecraft block is only a solid flag on PZ's square (`BlockBridge`), so PZ's own thumping never touches it; this does. Each thump: vanilla's breaking stage for the block (`ServerLevel.destroyBlockProgress`, one crack per block; the native renderer already draws Minecraft's cracks, `EntitySceneExporter` `blockBreakingRenderStates`), Minecraft's zombie door-attack sound (iron for metal blocks) and the block's hit sound, and `MSG_ZOMBIE_THUMP` (91) so PZ plays `ZombieThumpGeneric` at the zombie and makes the noise a thump makes (20 tiles, as `IsoThumpable.Thump`: more zombies come). Damage follows PZ's crowd rule: a thump counts fully when four or more zombies press at the block, a quarter for one. Health = vanilla hardness × 4 + blast resistance × 2, 2-600 thumps: glass 2, dirt 3, planks 14, stone 18, cobblestone/bricks 20, oak door 18, iron block 32, obsidian 600 (a crowd breaks cobblestone in about 8 s, one zombie in about 2 min). At the end `destroyBlock(pos, true)` (its drops, particles and sound; `BlockBridge` frees PZ's square as for any removed block). Left alone: unbreakable blocks, PzCraft's own blocks (trees), PZ's ground under a dug hole, blocks under the zombie's feet, blocks without collision, fluids. A crack heals after 30 s without a thump.
**PZ structures.** PZ's zombies already thump and break what a player builds in PZ (built walls, doors, windows, barricades: `IsoThumpable`). The walls and fences from W6's creative tabs were placed like map tiles (Brush Tool way), which PZ's zombies cannot break; they are now built as PZ builds a player's wall (`PzCraft_Moveables.lua` `placeWall`: `IsoThumpable.new`, ISWoodenWall's/`buildUtil.setInfo`'s settings: thumpable, not passable, thumpDmg 8, dismantable, low fences vaultable, break sound `BreakObject`, `AddSpecialObject`, `RecalcAllWithNeighbours`, `transmitCompleteItemToClients`). Health by the sprite's material (PZ's built wooden wall is 200-700): wood 400, plaster 250, metal 600, heavy metal 900, brick/stone/ceramic 800, glass/fabric/natural 150; fences half (low) or three quarters (tall). The Brush-Tool `placeTile` Lua is gone. W2b mining handles these walls and fences (thumpable edge walls, hoppable fences).

### 2026-10-16 17:34 CST — PZ's own right-click menus for Steve, on G (Claude, VPS; built and checked, not run in game)

Owner, after the list of PZ features Steve cannot use yet: "we can do what most important first that makes it playable and seamlessly. survivor mode will be in future." The most important was #1 of that list: PZ's world and item menus (fill water, light a stove, barricade, plumb, forage, radios, generators, eat/read/open/craft an item, ...), which Steve could not reach because his right-click is Minecraft's use.

**How it works:**
- **G** (PZ window key callback, `GuiInput.key`, only while Steve drives, the view has the mouse and PZ is not paused): `PzMenus.request`. While `PzMenus.holdsCursor()`, `Patch_GuiCapture` releases Viewpoint's capture as it does for a Minecraft screen; Viewpoint puts the cursor in the middle of the screen (where the crosshair was) and its 3D mouse pick (`MouseTargets`, read through `ViewpointBridge.mousePickOn()` by reflection) answers PZ's `ContextPick`. `PzMenus.tick` (game thread, `FrameTick`) waits for the pick (up to 12 frames once the cursor is free), then calls `PzCraftMenus.openWorld` (Lua), which runs PZ's own `ISObjectClickHandler.doRClick` with `UIManager.getPicked().tile`: the exact menu a right-click opens in Viewpoint's cursor mode. Minecraft gets no input meanwhile (`InputForwarder` is live only while captured).
- **Shift+G**, or G when nothing PZ knows is under the crosshair: `PzCraftMenus.openItem` runs `ISInventoryPaneContextMenu.createMenu` for the PZ item in Steve's main hand (Minecraft now sends its PZ id as `held.pzId` in the GUI state, `GuiBridge`; else the first item of that type in the hidden PZ inventory). Nothing at all: halo "Nothing to use there" / "Hold a Zomboid item".
- The cursor stays free while the menu (or a submenu) is visible, then while a window the chosen entry opened is up (a new visible UI element of at least 150x80 that was not there when the menu opened); then Viewpoint takes the mouse back by itself. **G again** gives the mouse back at any time (not while a PZ text box has focus, so G can be typed).
- `PzCraft_Menus.lua` (new, client): while Steve drives, PZ's walk-before-the-action (`ISWalkToTimedAction`, `ISWalkToTimedActionF`, `ISPathFindAction` with all its goals: location, nearest, furniture, corpse, car area/seat/side) no longer path-finds: it completes at once when the place PZ would walk to is within 3 tiles (4 for a car, same level ±1), so the queued action runs where Steve stands; farther away it says "Too far away" and stops (a path-find's own fail fallback still runs, as when PZ finds no path). `OnFillWorldObjectContextMenu` drops Walk to, Sit on ground, Sleep, Rest, Climb over/through, Climb sheet rope and the debug Get On Bed; `OnFillInventoryObjectContextMenu` drops Equip primary/secondary/both hands (Steve's hands are Minecraft's hotbar). These filters also apply to a right-click in Viewpoint's own cursor mode (middle mouse).
- Stats: `pzMenus` in the control API status (phase, worldMenus, itemMenus, nothing, windows, failures, last). README controls table: G and Shift+G.
- No protocol change (PZ reads the key itself; the item id rides the existing GUI state).

**Evidence (VPS):** `pz-mod` `sh gradlew build` OK, `Project-ZombCraft.jar` 475,076 B `4ab3773fb8d8cf66f41e8fa1797dd1252393b24644590c2d325e491fb79547d6`; `fabric-mod` `sh gradlew build` OK, `Project-ZombCraft-Fabric-0.13.0.jar` 1,041,138 B `58cf626a86c571290dc0b53e6aaca0cb78c070c3d383330d46a35900c20ea5c8`. ### 2026-10-16 18:04 CST — Viewpoint panel off for Steve, hand and Minecraft lighting, underground light, fire between the worlds (Claude, VPS; built and checked, not run in game)

Owner's list: "disable viewpoint auto interactive ui", "fix player and first person view of player during night is still bright", "fix lighting of minecraft lights", "setting fire on zombies doesnt show fire", "setting fire on buildings and ground spreads", "underground lighting is bright and doesn't correlate with pz/viewpoint shaders". Plan reviewed and approved ("Go ahead"); item 5 read as a request (fire set on PZ buildings and ground should spread), Viewpoint's panel off only while Steve plays.

**Causes found:**
- Viewpoint's loot/interact panel (`viewpoint.interact.LootMenu`) opens by itself on what the view aims at; nothing turned it off for Steve.
- The first-person hand (`NativeEntities.drawHands`) is drawn after the world with a fixed shade and no light at all: same brightness at noon and midnight.
- Minecraft geometry is lit only from PZ's light grid (`bandLight`). Under PZ's ground (level < 0) or beyond PZ's loaded squares Viewpoint falls back to full outdoor daylight (`farLight`: daylight, sky 1): caves as bright as a field, a torch there changes nothing. Above ground PZ knows no Minecraft walls or roofs: Minecraft rooms sky-lit, torch light through Minecraft walls. Minecraft's own light was never used. The Minecraft-light export made a PZ lamp of every emitting block in a 5x5-chunk area (up to 2048; every lava block), scanning every section twice a second.
- Minecraft fire on a PZ zombie's stand-in only arrived as plain damage; PZ never set the zombie burning; nothing drew flames (PZ's own fire on characters is a 2D attached sprite Viewpoint does not draw, and our renderer skipped the stand-ins). Correction to what I told the owner: the stand-ins do NOT burn in daylight; `ProxySunMixin` already stops that.
- Minecraft fire never reached PZ: flint and steel on a PZ surface placed nothing or a Minecraft fire that dies out.

**Changes:**
- (1) `ViewpointBridge.lootPanel` (from `SteveControl.update`): while Steve drives, `Viewpoint.Loot.setEnabled(false)` through `viewpoint.LootLua` (Viewpoint's public switch behind its Mod Options toggle), re-checked twice a second (its options can switch it back on); what Viewpoint had before is restored when Steve stops.
- (2) Hand shader: lit by `uLight`, the light PZ gives Steve's square (`IsoGridSquare.getLightInfo`, read each frame on the game thread, `NativeEntities.lightOn`); under a Minecraft roof or in a cave Minecraft's sky light caps it (times PZ's daylight), a Minecraft torch nearby adds its warm light; glowing parts stay bright. Dev command `handlight <gain>` (0.1-4, default 1) to match Viewpoint's exposure on maple; `handLight` in the status.
- (3)+(6) `protocol/VertexLight` (new, 4 tests): Minecraft's block and sky light packed into the vertex emission float (MARK 4096 + sky*256 + block*16 + emission in fifteenths; plain emission < 1 unchanged), so the shared vertex layouts stay as they are. Producers: `BlockSceneExporter.lightCoords` (light in front of a face; for direction-less quads the block's own, or its brightest neighbour when solid), `FluidCapture` (per vertex from the fluid renderer), `EntityCapture` (per vertex from models and particles, the submission's light for items and block models). Consumers: `NativeBlocks` vertex shader decodes; fragment: where PZ has no light data, Minecraft decides (sky light x PZ daylight, block light as warm torch light, a 0.012 floor); where PZ has data, sky = min(PZ, Minecraft), lamp = max(PZ, Minecraft torch), and under a Minecraft roof PZ's light is capped by Minecraft's sky. The same shader lights entities, particles and third-person Steve. `EnvironmentBridge`: Minecraft lights become PZ lamps only for the nearest 64 within 32 blocks at or above PZ's ground; sections without an emitter are skipped (`maybeHas`).
- (4)+(5) `protocol/Fire` (new, 5 tests): `MSG_IGNITE_ACTOR` 92, `MSG_IGNITE` 93 (`Ignite`: square, energy, anywhere), `MSG_FIRES` 94 (burning squares), `Wire.Actor.FLAG_BURNING` 32. PZ `FireBridge` (new): `SetOnFire` on the zombie or animal; `IsoFireManager.StartFire` where `IsoFire.CanAddFire` allows (PZ's sandbox fire rules then spread it); burning squares within 32 tiles (+-3 levels) sent twice a second when changed (5 s resend). `Gameplay` sets `FLAG_BURNING` from `isOnFire()`. Minecraft `PzFire` (new): a stand-in with Minecraft fire asks PZ to ignite its zombie (every 2 s at most); a PZ-burning zombie keeps its stand-in on fire so Minecraft's flames are drawn (`EntitySceneExporter` now submits invisible entities and stand-ins when they burn: only their flames render); stand-ins take no fire damage any more (`allowDamage`: IN_FIRE, ON_FIRE, CAMPFIRE refused; PZ's burn does the damage; lava still forwarded). Mixins (common): `FlintAndSteelMixin`, `FireChargeMixin` (on a PZ surface: PZ fire there, energy 50/80, burns anywhere; durability / item used as vanilla), `FirePlaceMixin` (every Minecraft fire block is watched; once a second those near Steve ask PZ to burn their square, where PZ finds something to burn, at most 32 per second and once per square per 5 s), `ArrowMixin` (a burning arrow stuck in PZ geometry: PZ fire, burns anywhere). PZ's fires: once a second each burning square has a 1-in-3 chance to set a flammable Minecraft block (`ignitedByLava`) in the 3x3 around it alight (only where `canSpreadFireAround`, Minecraft's fire-spread game rule); Steve standing in one catches fire for 3 s (not in water or rain). Single player for the fire bridge (PZ starts fires only on the server).

**Evidence (VPS):** protocol tests **87, 0 failures** (+FireTest 5, +VertexLightTest 4). Both mods build: PZ `Project-ZombCraft.jar` 485,630 B `455a1de5ea1e3e0c295b8a17d9b072b03504deb4321cd4db68189e5454411241`; Fabric `Project-ZombCraft-Fabric-0.13.0.jar` 1,058,192 B `ca507c814c60c01f0ca3b941a7e6064c812242ead1354effe95e67dfaa21e345`. `patchcheck` 56/0, 56/0. All five PzCraft Lua files compile with PZ's Kahlua. **Shaders compiled offline** with Khronos glslang 16.6.0 (scratch download) on the exact sources: `NativeBlocks` VERTEX, FRAGMENT (expanded with Viewpoint 0.1.6's own shader library and the `constants.glsl` its `Shaders.constants()` generates) and shadow fragment, `NativeEntities` hand vertex and fragment: all clean; a copy with an undeclared name fails (control). ### 2026-10-16 23:50 CST — B18 (the crash): guard in source, built, patchcheck clean; NOT run in game; cause still open (Claude, maple-pc)

Owner: "Start with B18, the crash". Nothing launched; no game running; nothing committed.

**What the code says:** PZ's `AttackState.triggerPlayerReaction(String, IsoGameCharacter)` (`javap -c -p` on `projectzomboid.jar`) takes `zombie.target`, and `checkcast`s it to `IsoPlayer`. So the NPE is Steve's own `IsoPlayer` with `getCurrentSquare()` null, inside `BodyDamage.UpdateBoredom` (called from `BodyDamage.Update`). `PuppetDriver.apply` (end of `IsoPlayer.postupdate`) moves Steve with `setForceX/Y/setZ` and only calls `setCurrent` when `findCurrentGridSquare()` is non-null, so it never nulls the square itself. A bytecode search for `setCurrent` with a null argument in `IsoMovingObject`, `IsoGridSquare`, `IsoGameCharacter`, `IsoPlayer`, `IsoZombie` found no path on the movement side (only `IsoWorld.KillCell` and `IsoZombie.resetForReuse`). Logs: Steve was placed at PZ (10720.50, 10195.50, 0) at 23:17:07 and was building/mining near (10790-10792, 10235) at 23:28:46-49.

**Change (pz-mod only):** `pz-mod/src/main/java/pzcraft/pz/BoredomGuard.java` (public helper: `skipTick(BodyDamage)` returns true, skipping PZ's boredom tick, when the character has no current square; logs the first and every 100th skip) and `Patch_BoredomSquare.java` (`@OnEnter(skipOn = true)` on `BodyDamage.UpdateBoredom` calling only the helper). The first version used `Log` and string concatenation in the advice; `tools/patchcheck.py` rejected it (4 access problems: advice code copied into PZ may only call public code), so the advice was moved into the public helper.

**Checks:** `gradlew build --offline` in `pz-mod` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (JDK 25). `Project-ZombCraft.jar` `BA96800F72361513B23C20EA8B5696E00913369ADA8B6B8A3A7C63A7391607E6` (487,176 B; the released zip's jar `40e04bae…` is 485,630 B and is what the owner approved). `python tools/patchcheck.py`: 57 patch classes, 0 problems. ### 2026-10-16 23:40-23:57 CST — B18 guard verified at the crash site; B31 (zombies never hit Steve in the test session) traced to the animation gate (Claude, maple-pc; autonomous through the PZ MCP)

**Guard (B18), verified:** `pz_lua`: `p:setCurrent(nil); p:getBodyDamage():UpdateBoredom()` in one call. Result: `UpdateBoredom_ok=true`, no error; `console.txt` and the debug log: `[Project-ZombCraft] boredom tick skipped: the character has no current square (1 so far; B18)` at 23:51:14; NPE count 0. So `BoredomGuard` works at the NPE site. Not verified: the full attack path, since no attack event fired (below).

**How to get zombies to act (from the earlier checks):** `tools/pz-mcp/check-jump-combat.py` uses `pz.cmd('pull 1.0')` and waits for `zombieAttacksForwarded` to rise (it did, in an earlier build). `UIManager.getSpeedControls():SetCurrentGameSpeed(1)` is the proper speed reset. `setGameSpeed(1)` also works. The Esc panel is `MainScreen.instance.javaObject`; `setVisible(false)` hides it.

**What was ruled out for B31 (zombies stand at contact and never attack):**
- Target: Steve's IsoPlayer (ID 4); `isTargetVisible()` true; both squares `isCouldSee(0)` true; `isVisibleToNPCs()` true; Steve not invisible, ghost or god.
- `spotted(p, true)` skips the `canSeeTarget` update; `spotted(p, false)` updates it, but the zombie stays in `PathFindState`.
- Forcing `AttackState` (`z:changeState(AttackState.instance())`): `isAttacking() = true`, the zombie stays in `AttackState` at 0.54 tiles for 40+ s, no hit, no damage forwarded.

**Where it stops:** the attack needs its animation event. `IsoGameCharacter.postUpdateAnimating` returns early unless `animationUpdatingThisFrame` is true; that field is set in an `updateAnimationTimeDelta` path with `allowsInvisibleAnimationSkips()` and `animationInvisibleFrameDelay`. Checked after that: `getAlpha(0) = 1` (the zombie is fully visible), so `updateAnimationTimeDelta` updates its animation every frame and the frame-delay throttle is **not** the cause. `animationUpdatingThisFrame` stays true. Next: the AdvancedAnimator's attack state (why `AttackState.animEvent` is never called for it), and whether our Minecraft-side zombie proxies (`PzCraftProxy`, `NativeEntities`) change anything about those zombies' animations.

**Repo check (added 23:58, `tools/pz-mcp/check-zombie-attack.py`, run through `pzcore`; evidence `tools/pz-mcp/zombie-attack-20261016-235845/result.json`):** pulled zombie 147 to 1.00 tile and sampled 30 s. Zombie stays in `AttackState`, `isAttacking() = true`, alpha 1, `AttackType = bite` set, `attackOutcome = start`, `bAttack = false`, `AttackDidDamage = false`. No hit (`zombieAttacksForwarded` 0), NPE count unchanged (0), skip count unchanged (1). So PZ sets the bite request and the animator never moves into the attack clip, which is what would fire `animEvent`. Speed type 3. Next: why the animator ignores `AttackType=bite` for this zombie (missing clip set for this zombie type, or the animator not stepping its state graph), and whether the same zombie stalls when not spawned or pulled by us.

**Not verified:** the full attack path (B18 end to end); whether a normal zombie fight in this session gets the same stall.
**Left in the game:** 5 spawned zombies around Steve (`spawn 2 4`, `spawn 3 2`), one of them (id 147) forced into `AttackState` at 0.54 tiles; the ingame Esc panel is hidden and game speed is 1 (the owner may want the menu back).
**Next step:** trace the animation gate (above). Then retry the attack at the crash spot, and watch `console.txt` for `boredom tick skipped` and any NPE.

**Found and fixed (details per bug in `release/BUGS.md`):**
- B21 sitting: `PzSeat` was registered `noSave()`; Minecraft 26.3's server refuses riding a non-serializable entity type, so no one could sit (car seats too). Now serializable but never saved (`shouldBeSaved`/`saveAsPassenger` false). And every PZ seat has PZ's bed flag, so at night chairs were beds: now a seat is sat on, a second right-click while sitting at night lies down. `PlayerState.FLAG_SEATED` keeps PZ's character on the seat's storey (feet hang 0.6 below the seat).
- B29 stairs: headroom. The storey above's floor slab over the tile before the stairs, and a wall on the stairwell's end edge, met Steve's head on the first steps. Protocol `CollisionSection.STAIRWELL` (+`TRIM_*`/`LIFT_*` in `heights`): those slabs stop 0.3 short of the stairwell, those walls start 0.6 above their floor (`CollisionBoxes`, `WorldExporter.stairwell`, 2 new tests).
- B24 items: Minecraft's item atlas with PZ's icons is 8192x4096 = the whole 128 MiB shared entity-texture space, so it never got to PZ. Textures over 32 MiB are now sent at half size (`EntitySceneExporter.readBack`/`shrink`).
- B28 roofs: Minecraft had no roof collision. `RoofShapes` (new) samples Viewpoint's own roof pieces (`MapRoofs.piece`, `RoofPiece.height`, reflection) and `WorldExporter` sends roof squares as 4x4 ramps. Squares Viewpoint fills in without a PZ roof sprite keep their flat floor (limit).
- B25/B26: block meshes carry Minecraft's light baked in and were re-exported only at the change and every 1 s; three follow-up exports at 0.2 s now catch the light update (`BlockSceneExporter`). Lag not reproduced.

**Verified in game (evidence in BUGS.md):** B32 (screenshots: Minecraft's hotbar only); B22 (attached flame sprites 1 → 0 on a burning zombie); B23 (stand-in captured as `submitFlame` only, 12 triangles); B20 (`tools/pz-mcp/check-creative-gun.py`: 5 creative shots, pistol stays 15+1); B19 (one click picks up a two-tile table); B30 (`block placed at tile (10834,10179) level 1`); B29 (up and down the staircase that blocked); B21 (sits by day, flags 48, PZ z 0.0005; lies down on a second click at night); B24 (`items.png -> slot 3 (4096x2048, from 8192x4096)`, held item visible); B28 roofs (lands and walks on the porch roof slope up to the ridge). ### 2026-10-17 14:26 CST — owner's post-Codex report: textures, items in craters, cars through the ground (Claude, maple-pc; built, NOT installed, NOT run in game)

Owner, after Codex's 13:52 install, with three screenshots: "minecraft textures they're downscaled too much"; "some gaps showing in minecraft and pz. maybe pz has elevation"; "pz items should go drop down elevation"; "pz vehicles clips through minecraft blocks underground"; "vic point blank mod is installed but cant use ... backport up to 1.20.1 ... modding API for project zombcraft". Both games were closed at the start (only Prism's launcher window and a Gradle daemon). Owner save untouched.

**1. Textures (fixed in source, built).** Cause from the owner's Prism `latest.log` 13:49:47: `entity texture minecraft:textures/atlas/items.png -> slot 3 (4096x2048, from 8192x4096)`. The item atlas holds PZ's items too, so B24's fix (shrink anything over 32 MiB) halved it and every Minecraft item drew 16 px art with 8 px (the apple in screenshot 1). Block atlas (2048x2048) was not affected. `protocol/EntityLink`: `TEXTURE_DATA_BYTES` 128->256 MiB, `VERSION` 5->6 (new shm file name; the old v5 file in %TEMP% can be deleted); `EntitySceneExporter.BIG_TEXTURE_BYTES` 32->128 MiB, so the atlas goes at 8192x4096; only a still bigger atlas is shrunk. `tools/pz-mcp/capture-weapon-view.py` also looks for v6. PZ copies the texture once per change (128 MiB direct buffer, PZ runs `-Xmx6072m`).

**3. PZ items in craters (implemented, built).** Cause: PZ's `GroundBridge.dig` (and blast floor removal) spills container contents onto the tile at floor height, and nothing lowered them. MC `GroundBridge`: per dug ground column, reports the top of the solid ground (`surface()`: collision-shape top, virtual ground counts) as `Wire.encodeGroundSurface(x, z, mcY)` (MSG_GROUND_EDIT flag `GROUND_SURFACE`=2, 13 bytes; old 9-byte edits unchanged), on every edit and in a once-a-second sweep of +-4 chunks (so craters dug before this build are reported too); only changes are sent, reset when PZ disconnects. PZ `ItemFall`: stores surfaces in mod data `PzCraftGroundSurface` (saved with the world), and every 15 frames sets falling every world item on such a tile (z=0, no floor) to `zoff = mcY / 2.449` with Minecraft gravity; a restored floor or filled hole lifts them back. Control stats `itemFall`, MC stats `surfaceReports`/`surfacesKnown`.

**4. Cars through the ground (implemented, built).** Cause: Bullet builds a square's physics from its flags; a dug tile has no `solidfloor`, so no `Floor` shape and a car falls into the Minecraft ground Bullet does not know. A car cannot properly go down into the hole: PZ takes its storey from its height (`floor(y/2.449+0.05)`), and storey -1 has no squares there (the missing-square crash class). `VehicleFloors` + `Patch_VehicleFloor` (OnExit of private `IsoChunk.calcPhysics`) add a `Floor` shape at ground level to hole tiles (ItemFall's surfaces) within 14 tiles of any car at storey 0; chunk physics refreshed with `checkPhysicsLater(0)` when the set changes. Cars therefore drive across holes at ground level. Limited to near cars so blast ragdolls still land in craters. Control stats `vehicleFloors`.

**Checks:** `pz-mod gradlew build` OK, `fabric-mod gradlew build` OK, protocol `gradlew test` 93 tests / 0 failures / 0 errors (new `groundSurfaceRoundTrips`), `patchcheck.py --viewpoint <Steam Viewpoint.jar> --pz projectzomboid.jar`: 63 patch classes 0 access problems, 63 targets 0 problems. Jars: PZ `pz-mod/build/libs/Project-ZombCraft.jar` `2597c513e5b3587455ff19b02791363bf5f25fe2c8e36b6ebf86e06ace03dd35` (531,306 B), Fabric `Project-ZombCraft-Fabric-0.16.2.jar` `4dd1f7a01338dc6d5d668353c39b9577f6fc851112c69859e72e6893caf5b68b` (1,088,086 B). Both must be installed together (EntityLink v6).

**Not done:** (2) the grey band along crater rims (screenshot 2) is not diagnosed: Viewpoint's floor is a flat quad (no slab edge), so it needs a live look; (5) back-port/mod API not started. **Next:** install both on the regression copy `CodexRegression_20261017`, check an item in hand at full resolution, TNT next to a container (items fall), and drive a car onto a crater; then look at a crater rim for the band.

### 2026-10-17 15:47 CST — Point Blank item atlas at full resolution: protocol v7, used-part upload (Claude side session, maple-pc; built only, NOT installed, NOT run in game)

Follows the 15:40 entry ("Found": with Point Blank 2.2.0 the item atlas is 8192x8192, 256 MiB, and went to PZ as `4096x4096, from 8192x8192`). Not specific to 26.1.2: shared sources.
**Change.** `protocol/EntityLink`: `VERSION` 6->7 (new shm `PzCraft_entities_v7.shm`; the v6 file in %TEMP% can be deleted), `TEXTURE_DATA_BYTES` 256->512 MiB (a whole 8192x8192 atlas plus as much again for skins/particles/equipment); texture slots add `+32 full width`, `+36 full height`; `Texture` record gains `fullWidth`/`fullHeight`; new `writeTexture(slot, w, h, stride, fullW, fullH, pixels)` sends the top-left `w x h` of a `fullW x fullH` texture whose other pixels are all 0 (old 5-argument `writeTexture` = full size). Fabric `EntitySceneExporter`: the fit/start-over/shrink decision moved after the GPU read-back (`publish`); `usedExtent` finds the smallest top-left box outside which every pixel is exactly 0 (any byte, so lossless; ~30-64 ms for 8192x8192, once per atlas change), only that part crosses; `BIG_TEXTURE_BYTES` 128->256 MiB applies to the used part; `shrink` takes a stride. Log line now `entity texture <id> -> slot N (8192x8192, sent WxH)` (", from ..." only if still shrunk). PZ `NativeEntities.upload`: full-size texture when the part is smaller: `glTexImage2D(full, null)`, `glTexSubImage2D` of the part, the rest zeroed in strips from a 4 MiB zero buffer (UVs unchanged; VRAM 256 MiB for an 8192x8192 atlas). `tools/pz-mcp/capture-weapon-view.py` knows v6/v7 (its size map lacked 6).
**Checks:** protocol `gradlew test` 95/0/0 (new `pointBlankItemAtlasFitsWholeWithRoomForTheRest`, `croppedTextureKeepsItsFullSize`); scratch check of `usedExtent` against brute force on 3000 random textures, 0 mismatches. `fabric-mod gradlew build` OK (both); javap: both jars contain `publish`/`usedExtent`, 26.1.2 uses `TextureFormat.pixelSize`, 26.3 `GpuFormat.blockSize`. `pz-mod gradlew build` OK. ### 2026-10-17 20:47 CST — 26.1.2: Minecraft window no longer shows (Claude side session, maple-pc)

**Owner report (16:3x):** "26.1.2 instance showing minecraft window and not being headless/integrated in project zomboid". **Cause:** 26.3 creates its SDL window `SDL_WINDOW_HIDDEN|SDL_WINDOW_NOT_FOCUSABLE`. The 26.1.2 port (`HiddenWindowMixin`, GLFW) only hinted `GLFW_VISIBLE=false` and `FOCUS_ON_SHOW=false`. After Steve's release, `WindowMode.set(false)` showed and iconified it, which left a normal app window: measured extended style `0x40110` (`WS_EX_APPWINDOW`), so it had a taskbar button and an Alt+Tab entry, and clicking either brought Minecraft up over PZ. Before the release it was correctly hidden (`window-states-before-fix.log`).
**Fix (26.1.2 only, `//#if MC_26_1`; 26.3 bytecode unchanged apart from line numbers):** `HiddenWindowMixin.pzcraft$noTaskbar` (RETURN of `Window.createGlfwWindow`, Windows only, unless `-Dpzcraft.visibleWindow`): `WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW|WS_EX_NOACTIVATE`, `WS_EX_APPWINDOW` removed, `SetWindowPos(FRAMECHANGED)`. `WindowMode.minimizeOffScreen` (Windows): instead of `glfwShowWindow`+`glfwIconifyWindow`, a single `SetWindowPlacement(SW_SHOWMINNOACTIVE, WPF_SETMINPOSITION -32000,-32000)`. Without it Windows parks a minimized window that has no taskbar button as a caption bar at the desktop's bottom-left (measured rect `0,1012,160,1040` in the intermediate build), and show+iconify flashed it at full size for one frame.
**Verified** (26.1.2 dev client `gradlew :mc26.1.2:runClient`, Point Blank 2.2.0; PZ via `run-pz.bat` with `PZ_SKIP_INSTALL=1` + private zb-dev profile; save `CodexRegression_20261017`; window state read every ~1 s with user32): created hidden with style `0x8000190`; hidden through title screen and world load; after Steve's release (20:45:4x) `visible=True iconic=True rect=-32000,-32000,...`, style unchanged (no APPWINDOW, so no taskbar/Alt+Tab), never foreground. Minecraft kept rendering and publishing (88.5 rendered/s, 88.5 published/s), link ready, and the PZ screenshot shows Minecraft's world + HUD inside PZ (`release/evidence/20261017/claude-26.1.2-window/`). **Not verified:** the Prism launch itself after install (same jar code as tested), and clicking in PZ for a long session.
**Builds:** `fabric-mod gradlew build` OK (both). Codex's 20:11 build already contained this source: 26.1.2 `6867c518b4865e5fdfcd…` (installed now), 26.3 `979f9cc348d8…` (Codex installed it in Prism 26.3; not changed here).
**State:** test games killed after PZ saved. Clone and `versions/26.1.2/run/saves` restored from `E:/PzCraftTest/backup-before-window-test2-20261017-2038` (diff identical); my interrupted 16:4x attempt had modified the clone before Codex's session. Installed PZ jar still `7f6c8b94…`. The previous 26.1.2 jar `2fe5ef1e…` is in `E:/PzCraftTest/backup-before-window-fix-install-20261017-2046`. Nothing committed. Note: the control API now answers 401 without its token (Codex), so use the pz tools or `pzcore`, not raw HTTP.
**Next:** the owner launches Prism "Project ZombCraft 26.1.2" with PZ: no Minecraft window or taskbar button should appear at any point.

### 2026-10-17 21:10 CST — owner report: 26.1.2 stuck UIs, MC sounds muting, 1.20.1 still open (Claude, maple-pc; built only)

**Owner (21:0x):** nested sessions stopped at the usage limit. Open: 26.1.x not integrated with PZ; MC sounds muted in PZ when too many sounds happen (revert to MC-played sounds acceptable if hard); 26.1.x not playable (cannot exit chat or inventory, stuck UIs, other bugs); 1.20.1 back-port not done. The owner wrote "26.1.3"; the only back-port target is `fabric-mod/versions/26.1.2`.
**Live state read only:** Prism 26.1.2 (pid 26448, our jar `6867c518…`) + PZ (pid 14076) running the owner's new save; MC log: link ready, Steve released 20:55:13, 89 rendered/published per second. PZ `console.txt` has no "Minecraft sounds stay in Minecraft", so the sound fallback never triggered.
**Cause (stuck UIs):** `GuiBridge.key()` maps PZ's GLFW key to 26.3's SDL `KeyEvent(scancode, keycode, mods)`. 26.1.2's `KeyEvent` record is `(int key, int scancode, int modifiers)` with GLFW key codes (javap of `minecraft-merged-deobf-26.1.2.jar`), so Escape arrived as key 41, Enter 40, Backspace 42, E 8: chat and inventory never closed, typing edits did not work. **Fix:** `//#if MC_26_1` returns `new KeyEvent(glfw, glfwGetKeyScancode(glfw), mods)`. 26.3 path unchanged.
**Cause (sound muting, inferred, not reproduced):** PZ creates FMOD with 1024 virtual channels; only a few dozen are mixed, the rest virtualized quietest-first. Our 2D one-shots played at default priority 128 with no cap, so bursts (mining, explosions, fights) filled the real slots. **Fix (`pz-mod` `SoundBridge`):** cap 24 concurrent (stop oldest), max 2 starts of the same file per frame, `FMOD_Channel_SetPriority(64)`, prune finished channels each frame; new stats `playing`, `virtualNow`, `cutOldest`, `mergedSameFrame` in `pzSounds`.
**Builds:** PZ `pz-mod/build/libs/Project-ZombCraft.jar` `c3386951552a…`; Fabric 26.3 `6a9e0769…`; Fabric 26.1.2 `e806895a47fd…` (javap: `glfwGetKeyScancode` present in `GuiBridge`). **Not installed, not run in game.** Install PZ + 26.1.2 (+ 26.3) jars together after the owner stops playing.

Files under `versions/1.20.1/src/main/{java,resources}` replace shared ones (as 26.1.2).

All shared files built: 845 distinct javac errors, 723 after the renames, in 70 of 135 files (+27 that only use those). Areas: (1) data components (`DataComponents`, `CustomData`, `net.minecraft.core.component`) → NBT on 1.20.1: PzItems, GunItem, MirrorBridge, ContainerBridge; (2) networking payloads (`CustomPacketPayload`, `StreamCodec`, `RegistryFriendlyByteBuf`, `PayloadTypeRegistry`) → Fabric networking v1 `FriendlyByteBuf` + channel ids; (3) capture half (render states, `SubmitNodeCollector`, `GpuTexture`/`getDevice()`, `RenderPassInfo`, `BlockStateModelPart`, `CameraRenderState`): EntityCapture 86, EntitySceneExporter 37, BlockSceneExporter 20, FrameCapture 12, GpuPriority 10 → rewrite against 1.20.1's `RenderSystem`/`RenderTarget`/`BufferBuilder`/`MultiBufferSource` and `glReadPixels`/PBO read-back; (4) HUD (`fabric...rendering.v1.hud`, `VanillaHudElements`, `GuiGraphicsExtractor`) → `HudRenderCallback` + `GuiGraphics`; (5) GeckoLib 5 → 4.x (GunItem, GunShells, PzWeaponModels); (6) game rules/world clocks (`GameRules` keys, `clockManager`, `defaultClock`) → `GameRules` + level day time: TimeBridge, WorldRules, ClockMixin; (7) item registration `Properties.setId(ResourceKey)` (1.21.2+), `getSelectedSlot()` → `inventory.selected`, `blockInteractionRange()` → reach 4.5/5; (8) input `KeyEvent`/`MouseButtonInfo` (1.21.9+) → `keyPressed(int,int,int)` / `mouseClicked(double,double,int)` (GLFW numbering, as the 26.1.2 GuiBridge fix); (9) `getDeltaTracker()` → `mc.getFrameTime()`; (10) ~43 `@Override` of methods that 1.20.1 lacks (mostly capture collector + mixin accessors).

**Owner report (23:0x, Prism 1.20.1):** two hands in first person; third person not working; underground not visible; creative/inventory incomplete. **Cause of the two hands:** the scene links (`SceneLink` blocks, `EntityLink` entities) are memory-mapped files that outlive the game. 1.20.1 never wrote them, so PZ kept drawing the last 26.x session's block mesh and hand batch (`nativeEntities.handBatches` 1, frame age 80 min; `NativeEntities.drawHands` draws whenever native blocks are active) next to 1.20.1's own video-layer hand (`hands-before-crop.png`). **Fix:** `versions/1.20.1/.../BlockSceneExporter.java` (stub) withdraws the block mesh (`SceneLink.invalidate`) and publishes an empty entity frame once a second while PZ is connected. **Verified (dev client + PZ, save copy):** one hand (`hands-after.png`), `nativeBlocks.active` false, `handBatches` no longer drawn. **Consequence found:** with the stale scene gone PZ uses the video world layer: Minecraft's blocks (materialised ground) are drawn flat over PZ and PZ's picture looks washed out (`hands-after.png`). On 26.x the native block/entity export (BlockSceneExporter, EntitySceneExporter, NativeTerrainMixin, EntityCapture) does this; porting it to 1.20.1 is what items 1–3 really need (underground, third-person body, correct depth). Jar `77ebb4d32091…` built only, not installed.
